+    <script id="theme-bootstrap">
+      (function () {
+        var key = 'auth-classroom:theme:v1';
+        var preference = 'system';
+        try {
+          var stored = localStorage.getItem(key);
+          if (stored === 'light' || stored === 'dark' || stored === 'system') {
+            preference = stored;
+          }
+        } catch (_) {}
+        var dark = preference === 'dark' || (preference === 'system' && typeof matchMedia === 'function' && matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches);
+        var theme = dark ? 'dark' : 'light';
+        document.documentElement.dataset.theme = theme;
+        document.documentElement.style.colorScheme = theme;
+        var meta = document.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]');
+        if (meta) meta.setAttribute('content', dark ? '#0f1522' : '#f5f3ed');
+      })();
+    </script>
   </head>

+import { AppShell } from './components/AppShell';
+import { ProgressProvider } from './context/ProgressContext';
+import { Dashboard } from './pages/Dashboard';

     <HashRouter>
-      <HomePage />

+        <Routes>
+          <Route element={<AppShell />}>
+            <Route index element={<Dashboard />} />
+            <Route path="/lesson/:lessonId" element={<LessonPage />} />
+            <Route path="/practice" element={<PracticePage />} />
+            <Route path="/demo" element={<DemoPage />} />
+            <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/" replace />} />

+        </Routes>
+      </ProgressProvider>
     </HashRouter>
   );

+import { fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
+import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+import { renderApp } from '../App';
+
+function renderAt(path: string) {
+  window.location.hash = `#${path}`;
+  return render(renderApp());
+}
+
+function primaryNavigation() {
+  return screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'นำทางหลัก' });
+}
+
+beforeEach(() => {
+  localStorage.clear();
+  window.location.hash = '#/';
+});
+
+afterEach(() => {
+  window.location.hash = '#/';
+  localStorage.clear();
+  vi.restoreAllMocks();
+});
+
+describe('AppShell navigation', () => {
+  it('prevents the skip-link fragment and focuses the main content', () => {
+    const scrollIntoView = vi.fn();
+    Object.defineProperty(Element.prototype, 'scrollIntoView', {
+      configurable: true,
+      value: scrollIntoView,
+    });
+    renderAt('/');
+    const skipLink = screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'ข้ามไปยังเนื้อหา' });
+    const main = document.getElementById('main-content');
+    if (!main) {
+      throw new Error('Main content was not rendered.');
+    }
+
+    skipLink.focus();
+    const click = new MouseEvent('click', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true });
+    fireEvent(skipLink, click);
+
+    expect(click.defaultPrevented).toBe(true);
+    expect(main).toHaveFocus();
+    expect(scrollIntoView).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ block: 'start' });
+    expect(scrollIntoView.mock.instances.at(-1)).toBe(main);
+    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#/');
+  });
+
+  it('marks home current on home and lesson routes and activates practice and demo', () => {
+    renderAt('/');
+
+    const homeLink = within(primaryNavigation()).getByRole('link', { name: 'หน้าหลัก' });
+    expect(homeLink).toHaveAttribute('aria-current', 'page');
+
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'เริ่มเรียน' }));
+    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#/lesson/overview');
+    expect(homeLink).toHaveAttribute('aria-current', 'page');
+    expect(within(primaryNavigation()).getByRole('link', { name: 'ฝึกท้ายบท' })).not.toHaveAttribute(
+      'aria-current',
+    );
+
+    fireEvent.click(within(primaryNavigation()).getByRole('link', { name: 'ฝึกท้ายบท' }));
+    expect(within(primaryNavigation()).getByRole('link', { name: 'ฝึกท้ายบท' })).toHaveAttribute(
+      'aria-current',
+      'page',
+    );
+    expect(within(primaryNavigation()).getByRole('link', { name: 'หน้าหลัก' })).not.toHaveAttribute(
+      'aria-current',
+    );
+
+    fireEvent.click(within(primaryNavigation()).getByRole('link', { name: 'Authentication Demo' }));
+    expect(
+      within(primaryNavigation()).getByRole('link', { name: 'Authentication Demo' }),
+    ).toHaveAttribute('aria-current', 'page');
+    expect(within(primaryNavigation()).getByRole('link', { name: 'ฝึกท้ายบท' })).not.toHaveAttribute(
+      'aria-current',
+    );
+  });
+
+  it('exposes the theme control and reserves desktop header space', () => {
+    renderAt('/');
+
+    expect(
+      screen.getByRole('button', { name: /ธีมปัจจุบันเป็นตามระบบ/ }),
+    ).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'เริ่มเรียน' })).toHaveClass(
+      'bg-brand-solid',
+      'text-on-inverse',
+    );
+    expect(screen.getByText('CLASSROOM')).toHaveClass('lg:hidden');
+  });
+
+  it('clips horizontal decoration without making the sticky shell a scroll container', () => {
+    const { container } = renderAt('/');
+    const wrapper = container.firstElementChild;
+    const navigation = container.querySelector('aside');
+
+    expect(wrapper).toHaveClass('overflow-x-clip');
+    expect(wrapper).not.toHaveClass('overflow-x-hidden');
+    expect(navigation).toHaveClass('sticky', 'top-0');
+  });
+
+  it('returns to the dashboard and resets focus and scroll after fragment navigation', async () => {
+    const scrollTo = vi.spyOn(window, 'scrollTo').mockImplementation(() => undefined);
+    renderAt('/#course-map');
+
+    try {
+      await waitFor(() => {
+        expect(document.getElementById('course-map')).toHaveFocus();
+      });
+      fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'AuthLab หน้าแรก' }));
+
+      await waitFor(() => {
+        expect(document.getElementById('main-content')).toHaveFocus();
+        expect(scrollTo).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ top: 0, behavior: 'instant' });
+      });
+      expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#/');
+    } finally {
+      scrollTo.mockRestore();
+    }
+  });
+});

+import {
+  ArrowRight,
+  BookOpen,
+  ClipboardCheck,
+  LayoutDashboard,
+  ShieldCheck,
+} from 'lucide-react';
+import { useEffect, type MouseEvent } from 'react';
+import { Link, Outlet, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
+import { getContinueSelection } from './ContinueCard';
+import { ThemeToggle } from './ThemeToggle';
+import { useClassroomProgress } from '../context/ProgressContext';
+import { getLessonsForLevel } from '../data/course';
+import type { Level } from '../types/course';
+
+const navigation = [
+  { to: '/', label: 'หน้าหลัก', icon: LayoutDashboard },
+  { to: '/practice', label: 'ฝึกท้ายบท', icon: ClipboardCheck },
+  { to: '/demo', label: 'Authentication Demo', icon: ShieldCheck },
+] as const;
+
+const levelLabels: Record<Level, string> = {
+  certificate: 'ปวช.',
+  diploma: 'ปวส.',
+};
+
+function skipToContent(event: MouseEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>) {
+  event.preventDefault();
+
+  const main = document.getElementById('main-content');
+  main?.focus();
+  main?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'start' });
+}
+
+export function AppShell() {
+  const { level, progress } = useClassroomProgress();
+  const location = useLocation();
+  const navigate = useNavigate();
+  const lessons = getLessonsForLevel(level);
+  const completedCount = progress.completed[level].length;
+  const continueSelection = getContinueSelection(lessons, progress.completed[level]);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    const target =
+      location.hash === '#course-map'
+        ? document.getElementById('course-map')
+        : document.getElementById('main-content');
+
+    if (location.hash === '#course-map') {
+      target?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'start' });
+    } else {
+      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'instant' });
+    }
+    target?.focus({ preventScroll: true });
+  }, [location.key, location.pathname, location.hash]);
+
+  return (
+    <div className="relative isolate min-h-screen overflow-x-clip bg-canvas font-sans text-ink">
+      <div
+        aria-hidden="true"
+        className="classroom-grid pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-0 top-0 -z-10 h-[680px] opacity-55"
+      />
+      <div
+        aria-hidden="true"
+        className="pointer-events-none absolute -right-32 top-28 -z-10 h-96 w-96 rounded-full bg-brand/10 blur-3xl"
+      />
+
+      <a
+        href="#main-content"
+        onClick={skipToContent}
+        className="fixed left-4 top-4 z-50 -translate-y-24 rounded-xl bg-ink px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-surface shadow-xl transition-transform focus:translate-y-0"
+      >
+        ข้ามไปยังเนื้อหา
+      </a>
+
+      <aside className="sticky top-0 z-40 border-b border-line/80 bg-surface/95 backdrop-blur-xl lg:fixed lg:inset-y-0 lg:left-0 lg:flex lg:h-screen lg:w-72 lg:flex-col lg:border-b-0 lg:border-r">
+        <div className="flex h-20 shrink-0 items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-line/80 px-5 sm:px-6">
+          <Link
+            to="/"
+            className="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3 rounded-xl"
+            aria-label="AuthLab หน้าแรก"
+          >
+            <span className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-ink text-surface shadow-sm">
+              <BookOpen aria-hidden="true" className="h-5 w-5" strokeWidth={1.8} />
+            </span>
+            <span className="text-xl font-bold tracking-[-0.03em]">AuthLab</span>
+            <span className="hidden rounded-full border border-line px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold tracking-[0.16em] text-muted sm:inline lg:hidden">
+              CLASSROOM
+            </span>
+          </Link>
+
+          <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2">
+            <ThemeToggle />
+            {continueSelection && (
+              <button
+                type="button"
+                onClick={() => navigate(`/lesson/${continueSelection.lesson.id}`)}
+                aria-label="เริ่มเรียน"
+                className="inline-flex min-h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-brand-solid px-3.5 text-xs font-semibold text-on-inverse transition-colors hover:bg-brand-solid-hover sm:w-auto lg:hidden"
+              >
+                <span className="hidden sm:inline">เริ่มเรียน</span>
+                <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
+              </button>
+            )}
+          </div>
+        </div>
+
+        <nav
+          aria-label="นำทางหลัก"
+          className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto border-b border-line/80 p-3 sm:px-5 lg:shrink-0 lg:flex-col lg:overflow-visible lg:border-b-0 lg:px-4 lg:py-6"
+        >
+          {navigation.map((item) => {
+            const Icon = item.icon;
+            const active =
+              item.to === '/'
+                ? location.pathname === '/' || location.pathname.startsWith('/lesson/')
+                : location.pathname.startsWith(item.to);
+
+            return (
+              <Link
+                key={item.to}
+                to={item.to}
+                aria-current={active ? 'page' : undefined}
+                className={`inline-flex min-h-11 shrink-0 items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3.5 text-sm font-medium transition-colors lg:w-full ${
+                  active
+                    ? 'bg-soft-brand font-semibold text-brand'
+                    : 'text-muted hover:bg-canvas hover:text-ink'
+                }`}
+              >
+                <Icon aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" strokeWidth={1.8} />
+                {item.label}
+              </Link>
+            );
+          })}
+        </nav>
+
+        <div className="hidden min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col lg:flex">
+          <div className="mx-5 mt-2 rounded-2xl border border-line bg-canvas p-4">

+  );

+import { CircleCheckBig, Info, TriangleAlert } from 'lucide-react';
+import type { LessonCallout } from '../types/course';
+
+const toneStyles = {
+  info: { label: 'ข้อมูล', className: 'border-brand/20 bg-soft-brand text-brand', Icon: Info },
+  warning: { label: 'คำเตือน', className: 'border-coral/25 bg-soft-coral text-coral', Icon: TriangleAlert },
+  success: { label: 'สำเร็จ', className: 'border-success-border bg-soft-success text-success', Icon: CircleCheckBig },
+} as const;
+
+export function Callout({ tone, title, text }: LessonCallout) {
+  const { label, className, Icon } = toneStyles[tone];
+
+  return (
+    <aside className={`rounded-xl border p-4 ${className}`}>
+      <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold">
+        <Icon aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
+        {label}
+      </p>
+      <h4 className="mt-2 text-sm font-semibold text-ink">{title}</h4>
+      <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-7 text-ink">{text}</p>
+    </aside>
+  );
+}

+import { fireEvent, render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+import { Callout } from './Callout';
+import { CodeExplorer } from './CodeExplorer';
+import type { CodeSnippet } from '../types/course';
+
+const snippet: CodeSnippet = {
+  id: 'test-app-js',
+  filename: 'auth-backend/src/app.js',
+  language: 'javascript',
+  caption: 'ตัวอย่าง middleware สำหรับทดสอบ interaction',
+  code: 'import express from \'express\';\n\nconst app = express();',
+  notes: [
+    'นำเข้า Express เพื่อสร้างแอปและกำหนด middleware',
+    'เว้นบรรทัดเพื่อแยกส่วน imports จากส่วนสร้างแอป',
+    'สร้าง instance ของ Express เพื่อใช้รับ HTTP request',
+  ],
+};
+
+const originalClipboard = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(navigator, 'clipboard');
+const originalExecCommand = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(document, 'execCommand');
+
+afterEach(() => {
+  if (originalClipboard) {
+    Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'clipboard', originalClipboard);
+  } else {
+    Reflect.deleteProperty(navigator, 'clipboard');
+  }
+  if (originalExecCommand) {
+    Object.defineProperty(document, 'execCommand', originalExecCommand);
+  } else {
+    Reflect.deleteProperty(document, 'execCommand');
+  }
+});
+
+function renderExplorer(value = snippet) {
+  return render(<CodeExplorer snippet={value} />);
+}
+
+describe('CodeExplorer', () => {
+  it('exports an explorer component', () => {
+    expect(CodeExplorer).toBeTypeOf('function');
+  });
+
+  it('shows file metadata, contained code, line numbers, and blank-line explanations', () => {
+    const { container } = renderExplorer();
+    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: snippet.filename })).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.getByText('javascript')).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.getByText(snippet.caption!)).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.getAllByRole('button', { name: /^บรรทัด \d+$/ })).toHaveLength(3);
+    expect(container.querySelector('.overflow-x-auto')).toHaveClass('max-w-full');
+    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'บรรทัด 1' })).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.getByText(snippet.notes[0])).toBeVisible();
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'บรรทัด 2' }));
+    expect(screen.getByText(snippet.notes[1])).toBeVisible();
+  });
+
+  it('updates the polite live detail panel through buttons and previous/next controls', () => {
+    renderExplorer();
+    const detail = screen.getByRole('complementary', { name: 'รายละเอียดบรรทัด' });
+    expect(detail).toHaveClass('bg-surface', 'text-ink', 'border-line');
+    expect(detail).not.toHaveClass('bg-white');
+    expect(detail.querySelector('[aria-live="polite"]')).not.toBeNull();
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'บรรทัด 3' }));
+    expect(within(detail).getByRole('heading', { name: 'บรรทัด 3' })).toBeVisible();
+    expect(within(detail).getByText(snippet.notes[2])).toBeVisible();
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'บรรทัดก่อนหน้า' }));
+    expect(within(detail).getByText(snippet.notes[1])).toBeVisible();
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'บรรทัดถัดไป' }));
+    expect(within(detail).getByText(snippet.notes[2])).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'บรรทัดถัดไป' })).toBeDisabled();
+  });
+
+  it('moves selection with ArrowUp and ArrowDown while the explorer is focused', () => {
+    renderExplorer();
+    const explorer = screen.getByRole('region', { name: `โค้ด ${snippet.filename}` });
+    explorer.focus();
+    fireEvent.keyDown(explorer, { key: 'ArrowDown' });
+    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'บรรทัด 2' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
+    fireEvent.keyDown(explorer, { key: 'ArrowDown' });
+    fireEvent.keyDown(explorer, { key: 'ArrowDown' });
+    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'บรรทัด 3' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
+    fireEvent.keyDown(explorer, { key: 'ArrowUp' });
+    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'บรรทัด 2' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
+    expect(screen.getByRole('complementary', { name: 'รายละเอียดบรรทัด' }).querySelector('[aria-live="polite"]')).toHaveTextContent(snippet.notes[1]);
+  });
+
+  it('resets to the first line when the snippet changes', () => {
+    const { rerender } = renderExplorer();
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'บรรทัด 3' }));
+    rerender(<CodeExplorer snippet={{ ...snippet, id: 'test-server-js', filename: 'server.js', code: 'console.log(1);', notes: ['แสดงเลข 1 ใน console เพื่อยืนยัน server file'] }} />);
+    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'บรรทัด 1' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
+    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'server.js' })).toBeVisible();
+  });
+
+  it('copies exact code and gives visible feedback', async () => {
+    const writeText = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined);
+    Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'clipboard', { configurable: true, value: { writeText } });
+    renderExplorer();
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'คัดลอกโค้ด' }));

+  });

+        'border-success-border',
+        'bg-soft-success',
+        'text-success',
+      );

+  });
+});

+import { ArrowDown, ArrowUp, FileCode2 } from 'lucide-react';
+import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
+import type { KeyboardEvent, ReactNode } from 'react';
+import Prism from 'prismjs';
+import type { Token, TokenStream } from 'prismjs';
+import 'prismjs/components/prism-bash';
+import 'prismjs/components/prism-sql';
+import 'prismjs/components/prism-json';
+import type { CodeLanguage, CodeSnippet } from '../types/course';
+import { CopyButton } from './CopyButton';
+
+function grammarFor(language: CodeLanguage) {
+  if (language === 'text') return undefined;
+  if (language === 'vue') return Prism.languages.markup;
+  return Prism.languages[language];
+}
+
+function renderTokens(tokens: TokenStream): ReactNode {
+  if (typeof tokens === 'string') return tokens;
+  if (Array.isArray(tokens)) {
+    return tokens.map((token, index) => <span key={index}>{renderTokens(token)}</span>);
+  }
+
+  const token = tokens as Token;
+  return (
+    <span className={`token ${[token.type, ...(Array.isArray(token.alias) ? token.alias : [token.alias])].filter(Boolean).join(' ')}`}>
+      {renderTokens(token.content)}
+    </span>
+  );
+}
+
+function HighlightedLine({ line, language }: { line: string; language: CodeLanguage }) {
+  const grammar = grammarFor(language);
+  const tokens = grammar ? Prism.tokenize(line, grammar) : line;
+  return <>{renderTokens(tokens)}</>;
+}
+
+export function CodeExplorer({ snippet }: { snippet: CodeSnippet }) {
+  const [selectedLine, setSelectedLine] = useState(0);
+  const lines = snippet.code.split('\n');
+  const lineCount = lines.length;
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    setSelectedLine(0);
+  }, [snippet.id]);
+
+  function selectLine(index: number) {
+    setSelectedLine(Math.max(0, Math.min(lineCount - 1, index)));
+  }
+
+  function handleKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLElement>) {
+    if (event.key === 'ArrowDown') {
+      event.preventDefault();
+      selectLine(selectedLine + 1);
+    } else if (event.key === 'ArrowUp') {
+      event.preventDefault();
+      selectLine(selectedLine - 1);
+    }
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <section
+      aria-label={`โค้ด ${snippet.filename}`}
+      tabIndex={0}
+      onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
+      className="min-w-0 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-line bg-[#111827] shadow-sm"
+    >
+      <header className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-white/10 bg-[#1f2937] px-4 py-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
+        <div className="flex min-w-0 items-start gap-3">
+          <FileCode2 aria-hidden="true" className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-indigo-300" />
+          <div className="min-w-0">
+            <h4 className="break-all font-mono text-sm font-semibold text-white">{snippet.filename}</h4>
+            <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
+              <span className="rounded-md bg-white/10 px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-indigo-200">
+                {snippet.language}
+              </span>
+              {snippet.caption && <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-slate-300">{snippet.caption}</p>}
+            </div>
+          </div>
+        </div>
+        <CopyButton text={snippet.code} />
+      </header>
+
+      <div className="grid min-w-0 items-start gap-0 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.4fr)_minmax(18rem,0.8fr)]">
+        <div className="min-w-0 max-w-full overflow-x-auto" tabIndex={0} aria-label="พื้นที่โค้ด เลื่อนแนวนอนได้">
+          <pre className="m-0 min-w-max bg-transparent p-0 font-mono text-[13px] leading-6 text-slate-100">
+            <code>
+              {lines.map((line, index) => (
+                <button
+                  key={`${snippet.id}-${index}`}
+                  type="button"
+                  aria-label={`บรรทัด ${index + 1}`}
+                  aria-pressed={selectedLine === index}
+                  onClick={() => selectLine(index)}
+                  className={`flex min-w-max items-start border-l-4 text-left transition-colors ${
+                    selectedLine === index
+                      ? 'border-indigo-400 bg-indigo-400/15'
+                      : 'border-transparent hover:bg-white/5'
+                  }`}
+                >
+                  <span
+                    aria-hidden="true"
+                    className={`sticky left-0 w-14 shrink-0 select-none px-3 py-2 text-right text-slate-400 ${
+                      selectedLine === index ? 'bg-[#172036] text-indigo-200' : 'bg-[#111827]'
+                    }`}
+                  >
+                    {index + 1}
+                  </span>
+                  <code className={`language-${snippet.language} block min-w-max whitespace-pre px-4 py-2`}>
+                    <HighlightedLine line={line} language={snippet.language} />
+                    {line.length === 0 ? '\u00a0' : null}
+                  </code>
+                </button>
+              ))}
+            </code>
+          </pre>
+        </div>
+
+        <aside
+          aria-label="รายละเอียดบรรทัด"
+          className="min-w-0 border-t border-line bg-surface p-5 text-ink lg:min-h-full lg:border-l lg:border-t-0"
+        >
+          <div aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true">
+            <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold tracking-[0.16em] text-brand">LINE NOTE</p>
+            <h4 id={`code-note-${snippet.id}`} className="mt-2 text-lg font-semibold">
+              บรรทัด {selectedLine + 1}
+            </h4>
+            <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-7 text-muted">
+              {snippet.notes[selectedLine] ?? 'ไม่มีคำอธิบายสำหรับบรรทัดนี้'}
+            </p>
+          </div>
+          <div className="mt-5 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
+            <button
+              type="button"
+              onClick={() => selectLine(selectedLine - 1)}
+              disabled={selectedLine === 0}
+              className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-line px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-ink hover:bg-canvas disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
+            >
+              <ArrowUp aria-hidden="true" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
+              บรรทัดก่อนหน้า
+            </button>
+            <button
+              type="button"
+              onClick={() => selectLine(selectedLine + 1)}
+              disabled={selectedLine === lineCount - 1}
+              className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-line px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-ink hover:bg-canvas disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
+            >
+              บรรทัดถัดไป
+              <ArrowDown aria-hidden="true" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
+            </button>
+          </div>
+          <p className="mt-4 text-xs leading-6 text-muted">
+            โฟกัส Code Explorer แล้วใช้ปุ่มลูกศรขึ้น/ลง เพื่อเปลี่ยนบรรทัดที่อ่าน
+          </p>
+        </aside>
+      </div>
+    </section>
+  );
+}

+import { fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
+import { MemoryRouter } from 'react-router-dom';
+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import { ContinueCard } from './ContinueCard';
+import { ProgressProvider } from '../context/ProgressContext';
+import { certificateLessons } from '../data/course';
+
+interface Rgb {
+  r: number;
+  g: number;
+  b: number;
+}
+
+import sourceStyles from '../index.css?raw';
+
+function parseHex(color: string): Rgb {
+  const hex = color.trim().replace('#', '');
+  if (!/^[\da-f]{6}$/i.test(hex)) {
+    throw new Error(`Expected a six-digit hex color, received ${color}.`);
+  }
+  return {
+    r: Number.parseInt(hex.slice(0, 2), 16),
+    g: Number.parseInt(hex.slice(2, 4), 16),
+    b: Number.parseInt(hex.slice(4, 6), 16),
+  };
+}
+
+function relativeLuminance(color: Rgb) {
+  const channels = [color.r, color.g, color.b].map((channel) => {
+    const value = channel / 255;
+    return value <= 0.04045 ? value / 12.92 : ((value + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4;
+  });
+  return channels[0] * 0.2126 + channels[1] * 0.7152 + channels[2] * 0.0722;
+}
+
+function contrastRatio(foreground: string, background: string) {
+  const foregroundLuminance = relativeLuminance(parseHex(foreground));
+  const backgroundLuminance = relativeLuminance(parseHex(background));
+  const lighter = Math.max(foregroundLuminance, backgroundLuminance);
+  const darker = Math.min(foregroundLuminance, backgroundLuminance);
+  return (lighter + 0.05) / (darker + 0.05);
+}
+
+type Theme = 'light' | 'dark';
+type ThemeColor =
+  | 'ink'
+  | 'coral'
+  | 'accent'
+  | 'accent-hover'
+  | 'on-accent'
+  | 'inverse'
+  | 'on-inverse';
+
+function themeColor(name: ThemeColor, theme: Theme = 'light') {
+  const selector = theme === 'dark'
+    ? /:root\[data-theme=["']dark["']\]\s*\{([^}]*)\}/
+    : /:root\s*\{([^}]*)\}/;
+  const block = sourceStyles.match(selector)?.[1];
+  const value = block?.match(new RegExp(`--${name}:\\s*(#[\\da-fA-F]{6})`))?.[1];
+  if (!value) {
+    throw new Error(`Theme color --${name} was not found in the ${theme} theme.`);
+  }
+  return value;
+}
+
+function ContinueLink() {
+  return (
+    <MemoryRouter>
+      <ProgressProvider>
+        <ContinueCard lessons={certificateLessons} />
+      </ProgressProvider>
+    </MemoryRouter>
+  );
+}
+
+beforeEach(() => {
+  localStorage.clear();
+});
+
+describe('ContinueCard CTA contrast', () => {
+  it.each(['light', 'dark'] as const)(
+    'uses dark on-accent text on the coral backgrounds in the %s theme',
+    (theme) => {
+      expect(contrastRatio(themeColor('on-accent', theme), themeColor('accent', theme))).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5);
+      expect(contrastRatio(themeColor('on-accent', theme), themeColor('accent-hover', theme))).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5);
+    },
+  );
+
+  it('keeps the rendered CTA semantic and at AA contrast for both theme palettes', () => {
+    render(<ContinueLink />);
+    const cta = screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'เริ่มบทแรก' });
+
+    expect(cta).toHaveClass('bg-accent', 'text-on-accent', 'hover:bg-accent-hover');
+    expect(cta).not.toHaveClass('text-white');
+    for (const theme of ['light', 'dark'] as const) {
+      expect(contrastRatio(themeColor('on-accent', theme), themeColor('accent', theme))).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5);
+      expect(contrastRatio(themeColor('on-accent', theme), themeColor('accent-hover', theme))).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5);
+    }
+
+    fireEvent.mouseOver(cta);
+    expect(cta).toHaveClass('text-on-accent', 'hover:bg-accent-hover');
+  });
+
+  it('keeps the continue panel dark with inverse foreground tokens in both themes', () => {
+    render(<ContinueLink />);
+    const panel = screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'เส้นทางถัดไปของคุณ' }).closest('section');
+
+    expect(panel).toHaveClass('bg-inverse', 'text-on-inverse');
+    for (const theme of ['light', 'dark'] as const) {
+      expect(contrastRatio(themeColor('on-inverse', theme), themeColor('inverse', theme))).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5);
+    }
+  });
+});

+import { ArrowRight, BookOpenCheck, Clock3 } from 'lucide-react';
+import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
+import { useClassroomProgress } from '../context/ProgressContext';
+import type { LessonId, LessonSummary } from '../types/course';
+
+export interface ContinueSelection {
+  lesson: LessonSummary;
+  label: 'เริ่มบทแรก' | 'เรียนต่อ' | 'ทบทวนบทสุดท้าย';
+}
+
+export function getContinueSelection(
+  lessons: LessonSummary[],
+  completed: LessonId[],
+): ContinueSelection | undefined {
+  const finalLesson = lessons.at(-1);
+  if (!finalLesson) {
+    return undefined;
+  }
+
+  const completedIds = new Set(completed);
+  const allComplete = lessons.every((lesson) => completedIds.has(lesson.id));
+  const lesson =
+    lessons.find((candidate) => !completedIds.has(candidate.id)) ?? finalLesson;
+
+  if (allComplete) {
+    return { lesson: finalLesson, label: 'ทบทวนบทสุดท้าย' };
+  }
+
+  return {
+    lesson,
+    label: completed.length === 0 ? 'เริ่มบทแรก' : 'เรียนต่อ',
+  };
+}
+
+export interface ContinueCardProps {
+  lessons: LessonSummary[];
+}
+
+export function ContinueCard({ lessons }: ContinueCardProps) {
+  const { level, progress } = useClassroomProgress();
+  const completed = progress.completed[level];
+  const selection = getContinueSelection(lessons, completed);
+
+  if (!selection) {
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <section
+      aria-labelledby="continue-title"
+      className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-[1.75rem] bg-inverse p-6 text-on-inverse shadow-[0_24px_60px_-32px_rgba(23,34,59,0.7)] sm:p-7"
+    >
+      <div
+        aria-hidden="true"
+        className="absolute -right-14 -top-16 h-44 w-44 rounded-full bg-brand/35 blur-3xl"
+      />
+      <div className="relative">
+        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
+          <span className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-white/10 text-accent">
+            <BookOpenCheck aria-hidden="true" className="h-5 w-5" strokeWidth={1.8} />
+          </span>
+          <div>
+            <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold tracking-[0.18em] text-on-inverse/55">
+              YOUR NEXT STEP
+            </p>
+            <h2 id="continue-title" className="mt-1 text-lg font-semibold">
+              เส้นทางถัดไปของคุณ
+            </h2>
+          </div>
+        </div>
+
+        <p className="mt-6 text-xl font-semibold leading-8 tracking-[-0.02em]">
+          {selection.lesson.title}
+        </p>
+        <p className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-on-inverse/65">
+          <Clock3 aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
+          บทที่ {selection.lesson.order} · {selection.lesson.durationMinutes} นาที
+        </p>
+
+        <Link
+          to={`/lesson/${selection.lesson.id}`}
+          className="group mt-6 inline-flex min-h-12 w-full items-center justify-center gap-3 rounded-xl bg-accent px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-on-accent transition-colors hover:bg-accent-hover focus-visible:outline-on-inverse sm:w-auto"
+        >
+          {selection.label}
+          <ArrowRight
+            aria-hidden="true"
+            className="h-4 w-4 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-0.5"
+          />
+        </Link>
+      </div>
+    </section>
+  );
+}

+import { Check, Copy } from 'lucide-react';
+import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
+
+interface CopyButtonProps {
+  text: string;
+}
+
+function fallbackCopy(text: string) {
+  const textarea = document.createElement('textarea');
+  textarea.value = text;
+  textarea.setAttribute('readonly', '');
+  textarea.style.position = 'fixed';
+  textarea.style.left = '-9999px';
+  document.body.appendChild(textarea);
+  textarea.select();
+
+  try {
+    if (!document.execCommand('copy')) {
+      throw new Error('Copy command was rejected');
+    }
+  } finally {
+    textarea.remove();
+  }
+}
+
+export function CopyButton({ text }: CopyButtonProps) {
+  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
+  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
+  const timer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined>(undefined);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    setCopied(false);
+    setFailed(false);
+    return () => clearTimeout(timer.current);
+  }, [text]);
+
+  async function handleCopy() {
+    try {
+      if (navigator.clipboard?.writeText) {
+        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
+      } else {
+        fallbackCopy(text);
+      }
+      setCopied(true);
+      setFailed(false);
+    } catch {
+      try {
+        fallbackCopy(text);
+        setCopied(true);
+        setFailed(false);
+      } catch {
+        setCopied(false);
+        setFailed(true);
+      }
+    }
+
+    clearTimeout(timer.current);
+    timer.current = setTimeout(() => {
+      setCopied(false);
+      setFailed(false);
+    }, 2400);
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <button
+      type="button"
+      onClick={handleCopy}
+      className="inline-flex min-h-10 shrink-0 items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-white/15 bg-white/10 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white transition-colors hover:bg-white/20"
+    >
+      {copied ? <Check aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" /> : <Copy aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />}
+      <span aria-live="polite">{copied ? 'คัดลอกแล้ว' : failed ? 'คัดลอกไม่สำเร็จ' : 'คัดลอกโค้ด'}</span>
+    </button>
+  );
+}

+            <span
+              className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold ${
+                completed ? 'bg-soft-brand text-brand' : 'bg-canvas text-muted'
+              }`}
+            >
+              {completed && <Check aria-hidden="true" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />}
+              {completed ? 'เสร็จแล้ว' : 'ยังไม่เริ่ม'}
+            </span>
+          </div>
+
+          <h3 className="mt-3 text-base font-semibold leading-7 tracking-[-0.015em] text-ink group-hover:text-brand sm:text-lg">
+            {lesson.title}
+          </h3>
+          <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-7 text-muted">{lesson.description}</p>
+
+          <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
+            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-line bg-canvas px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-muted">
+              <Clock3 aria-hidden="true" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
+              {lesson.durationMinutes} นาที
+            </span>
+            {lesson.tags.map((tag) => (
+              <span
+                key={tag}
+                className="rounded-lg border border-line px-2.5 py-1.5 font-mono text-[11px] font-medium text-muted"
+              >
+                {tag}
+              </span>
+            ))}
+          </div>
+        </article>
+      </Link>
+    </li>
+  );
+}

+++ b/src/components/PasswordChecklist.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,32 @@
+import { Check, X } from 'lucide-react';
+import { getPasswordChecks } from '../lib/authDemo';
+
+interface PasswordChecklistProps {
+  password: string;
+}
+
+export function PasswordChecklist({ password }: PasswordChecklistProps) {
+  const checks = getPasswordChecks(password);
+  const passedCount = checks.filter((check) => check.passed).length;
+
+  return (
+    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-line bg-canvas/70 p-4">
+      <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-ink">
+        ผ่าน {passedCount} จาก {checks.length} เงื่อนไข
+      </p>
+      <ul className="mt-3 grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2" aria-label="เงื่อนไขรหัสผ่าน">
+        {checks.map((check) => (
+          <li key={check.label} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted">
+            {check.passed ? (
+              <Check aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-brand" />
+            ) : (
+              <X aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-danger" />
+            )}
+            <span>{check.label}</span>
+            <span className="sr-only">{check.passed ? 'ผ่านแล้ว' : 'ยังไม่ผ่าน'}</span>
+          </li>
+        ))}
+      </ul>
+    </div>
+  );
+}
diff --git a/src/components/ThemeToggle.test.tsx b/src/components/ThemeToggle.test.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..2a9398d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/components/ThemeToggle.test.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,108 @@
+import { act, fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
+import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+import { ThemeToggle } from './ThemeToggle';
+import { THEME_STORAGE_KEY } from '../lib/theme';
+
+let systemMatches = false;
+let systemChange: ((event: MediaQueryListEvent) => void) | undefined;
+const addEventListener = vi.fn((_type: string, listener: (event: MediaQueryListEvent) => void) => {
+  systemChange = listener;
+});
+const removeEventListener = vi.fn();
+
+beforeEach(() => {
+  localStorage.clear();
+  systemMatches = false;
+  systemChange = undefined;
+  addEventListener.mockClear();
+  removeEventListener.mockClear();
+  Object.defineProperty(window, 'matchMedia', {
+    configurable: true,
+    value: vi.fn(() => ({
+      get matches() {
+        return systemMatches;
+      },
+      media: '(prefers-color-scheme: dark)',
+      addEventListener,
+      removeEventListener,
+    })),
+  });
+});
+
+afterEach(() => {
+  document.documentElement.removeAttribute('data-theme');
+  document.documentElement.removeAttribute('style');
+  localStorage.clear();
+});
+
+describe('ThemeToggle', () => {
+  it('cycles through light, dark, and system while persisting the selection', () => {
+    render(<ThemeToggle />);
+    const toggle = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /ธีม/i });
+
+    expect(toggle).toHaveTextContent('ตามระบบ');
+    expect(toggle).toHaveAttribute('aria-label', expect.stringContaining('ตามระบบ'));
+    expect(document.documentElement).toHaveAttribute('data-theme', 'light');
+    expect(localStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY)).toBe('system');
+
+    fireEvent.click(toggle);
+    expect(toggle).toHaveTextContent('สว่าง');
+    expect(document.documentElement).toHaveAttribute('data-theme', 'light');
+    expect(localStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY)).toBe('light');
+
+    fireEvent.click(toggle);
+    expect(toggle).toHaveTextContent('มืด');
+    expect(document.documentElement).toHaveAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');
+    expect(document.documentElement.style.colorScheme).toBe('dark');
+    expect(localStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY)).toBe('dark');
+
+    fireEvent.click(toggle);
+    expect(toggle).toHaveTextContent('ตามระบบ');
+    expect(document.documentElement).toHaveAttribute('data-theme', 'light');
+    expect(localStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY)).toBe('system');
+  });
+
+  it('restores a saved dark preference on the first render', () => {
+    localStorage.setItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, 'dark');
+
+    render(<ThemeToggle />);
+
+    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /ธีม/i })).toHaveTextContent('มืด');
+    expect(document.documentElement).toHaveAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');
+  });
+
+  it('reacts to operating-system changes only while following the system', () => {
+    render(<ThemeToggle />);
+    const toggle = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /ธีม/i });
+    expect(document.documentElement).toHaveAttribute('data-theme', 'light');
+
+    act(() => {
+      systemMatches = true;
+      systemChange?.({ matches: true } as MediaQueryListEvent);
+    });
+    expect(document.documentElement).toHaveAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');
+
+    fireEvent.click(toggle);
+    act(() => {
+      systemMatches = false;
+      systemChange?.({ matches: false } as MediaQueryListEvent);
+    });
+    expect(document.documentElement).toHaveAttribute('data-theme', 'light');
+    expect(localStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY)).toBe('light');
+  });
+
+  it('removes the system listener and tolerates blocked preference writes', () => {
+    vi.spyOn(Storage.prototype, 'setItem').mockImplementation(() => {
+      throw new DOMException('Storage blocked', 'SecurityError');
+    });
+    const { unmount } = render(<ThemeToggle />);
+    const toggle = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /ธีม/i });
+
+    expect(() => fireEvent.click(toggle)).not.toThrow();
+    expect(document.documentElement).toHaveAttribute('data-theme', 'light');
+
+    unmount();
+    expect(addEventListener).toHaveBeenCalledWith('change', expect.any(Function));
+    expect(removeEventListener).toHaveBeenCalledWith('change', expect.any(Function));
+  });
+});
diff --git a/src/components/ThemeToggle.tsx b/src/components/ThemeToggle.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..1e218a3
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/components/ThemeToggle.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,62 @@
+import { Monitor, Moon, Sun } from 'lucide-react';
+import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
+import {
+  applyTheme,
+  getSystemTheme,
+  readThemePreference,
+  resolveTheme,
+  writeThemePreference,
+  type ThemePreference,
+} from '../lib/theme';
+
+const preferences: ThemePreference[] = ['system', 'light', 'dark'];
+const labels: Record<ThemePreference, string> = {
+  system: 'ตามระบบ',
+  light: 'สว่าง',
+  dark: 'มืด',
+};
+const icons = {
+  system: Monitor,
+  light: Sun,
+  dark: Moon,
+};
+
+export function ThemeToggle() {
+  const [preference, setPreference] = useState<ThemePreference>(readThemePreference);
+  const [systemTheme, setSystemTheme] = useState(getSystemTheme);
+  const theme = resolveTheme(preference, systemTheme);
+  const Icon = icons[preference];
+  const nextPreference = preferences[(preferences.indexOf(preference) + 1) % preferences.length];
+  const actionLabel = `ธีมปัจจุบันเป็น${labels[preference]} เปลี่ยนเป็น${labels[nextPreference]}`;
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (typeof window.matchMedia !== 'function') {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const media = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)');
+    const handleChange = (event: MediaQueryListEvent) => {
+      setSystemTheme(event.matches ? 'dark' : 'light');
+    };
+    media.addEventListener('change', handleChange);
+    return () => media.removeEventListener('change', handleChange);
+  }, []);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    applyTheme(theme);
+    writeThemePreference(preference);
+  }, [preference, theme]);
+
+  return (
+    <button
+      type="button"
+      onClick={() => setPreference(nextPreference)}
+      aria-label={actionLabel}
+      title={actionLabel}
+      className="inline-flex min-h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface px-0 text-xs font-semibold text-muted transition-colors hover:border-brand/35 hover:text-brand sm:w-auto sm:px-3"
+    >
+      <Icon aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" strokeWidth={1.8} />
+      <span className="hidden sm:inline">{labels[preference]}</span>
+    </button>
+  );
+}
diff --git a/src/context/ProgressContext.tsx b/src/context/ProgressContext.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..008d197
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/context/ProgressContext.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,23 @@
+import { createContext, useContext } from 'react';
+import type { PropsWithChildren } from 'react';
+import { useProgress } from '../hooks/useProgress';
+
+type ClassroomProgress = ReturnType<typeof useProgress>;
+
+const ProgressContext = createContext<ClassroomProgress | undefined>(undefined);
+
+export function ProgressProvider({ children }: PropsWithChildren) {
+  const progress = useProgress();
+
+  return <ProgressContext.Provider value={progress}>{children}</ProgressContext.Provider>;
+}
+
+export function useClassroomProgress(): ClassroomProgress {
+  const progress = useContext(ProgressContext);
+
+  if (!progress) {
+    throw new Error('useClassroomProgress must be used within a ProgressProvider.');
+  }
+
+  return progress;
+}

+      summary: 'สรุปหลักฐาน test ที่รันจริงและยืนยันว่า negative paths ไม่รั่วข้อมูลหรือทำให้ UI ค้าง',
+      checklist: ['รัน Vitest และบันทึกผลลัพธ์', 'รัน API matrix ทั้งหกรายการ', 'ตรวจ wrong password/missing token/expired token', 'ทำ manual browser checklist ด้วย keyboard', 'ตรวจ Network และ log ไม่มี secrets'],
+    },
+  },
+};
+
+const diplomaNotes: Partial<Record<CoreLessonId, Partial<Record<string, string>>>> = {
+  overview: {
+    'login-session-flow': 'ระดับปวส. ต้องเปรียบเทียบ stateless JWT กับ server-side session ในแง่ horizontal scaling, immediate revocation, token size และการเก็บ secret; Demo นี้เลือก JWT เพื่อเรียนรู้ flow ไม่ใช่การยืนยันว่าเหมาะกับ production ทุกกรณี',
+    'http-json-contract': 'ออกแบบ contract ให้เพิ่ม request id และ standardized error codes สำหรับ tracing โดยไม่ใส่ stack traceหรือ internal SQL ใน response และตกลง versioning ก่อนเปลี่ยน schema',
+  },
+  installation: {
+    'install-mysql-heidi': 'เลือก MySQL/MariaDB เวอร์ชันที่ได้รับการสนับสนุนและทดสอบกับ mysql2 driver รวมถึง backup/restore, TLS และ least-privilege account; การติดตั้งผ่าน GUI ไม่ได้ยืนยันว่า environment พร้อม production',
+  },
+  setup: {
+    'environment-secrets': 'แยก secret/config ตาม environment และ inject ผ่าน secret manager/CI protected variables; ใช้ key rotation และ audit access แทนการคัดลอก .env ไปยัง production ทุกเครื่อง',
+    'vite-proxy': 'ใน production ใช้ reverse proxy/TLS termination, health checks, graceful timeout และ security headers ที่ตรงกับ deployment; Vite proxy เป็นเครื่องมือ development เท่านั้นและไม่ควรเปิดสู่ internet',
+  },
+  database: {
+    'parameterized-queries': 'Parameterized query ป้องกัน SQL injection แต่ไม่แก้ authorization; ต้องตรวจ ownership ทุก query, ใช้ transaction สำหรับ multi-step writes และป้องกัน race ด้วย unique/foreign key constraints',
+    'users-schema': 'พิจารณา email canonicalization, verification status, account lockout และ audit fields โดยไม่เพิ่มข้อมูลเกินความจำเป็น; schema เปลี่ยนผ่าน migration ที่ทดสอบ rollback ได้',
+  },
+  backend: {
+    'register-login-controllers': 'generic login error ลด enumeration แต่ยังอาจรั่วผ่าน timing, rate-limit response หรือ duplicate registration; ต้องทำ uniform timing, ข้อความ/สถานะสม่ำเสมอและประเมิน flow email verification',
+    'protected-me-route': 'JWT ที่ stateless ยกเลิกยาก ต้องวาง short expiry, refresh rotation, token version/family revocation และ incident response; key rotation ต้องรองรับ old/new signing keys ชั่วคราว',
+  },
+  api: {
+    'endpoints-token-flow': 'เพิ่ม idempotency/request correlation สำหรับ operation สำคัญและเอกสาร OpenAPI; ห้าม log Authorization header หรือ response token และกำหนด retry behavior สำหรับ 429/5xx ให้ชัดเจน',
+    'cors-rate-devtools': 'Rate limit ตาม IP ต้องรวม trust proxy, distributed store และ key ที่เหมาะกับ NAT; CORS เป็น browser policy ไม่ป้องกัน non-browser clients จึงต้องมี authentication และ authorization จริง',
+  },
+  frontend: {
+    'http-store': 'localStorage token เสี่ยง XSS; ระดับปวส. ต้องเปรียบเทียบ HttpOnly refresh cookie, in-memory access token, CSRF token และ Secure/SameSite cookie พร้อมแผนการ revoke เมื่อหมดอายุ',
+    'dashboard-logout': 'Client logout เป็นเพียงการล้าง state; production ต้อง revoke refresh token ฝั่ง server และจัดการ multi-tab synchronization, silent refresh และ race ระหว่าง refresh หลาย request',
+  },
+  testing: {
+    'test-matrix': 'ขยาย matrix เป็น integration tests ด้วย test database, concurrency duplicate registration, expired/revoked token, CORS origin และ rate limit; deterministic fixtures ต้องไม่พึ่ง production data',
+    'vitest-password-policy': 'ใช้ property-based/fuzz testing และ threat modeling เพื่อหา input ขอบเขต เช่น Unicode, byte length และ timing; unit tests ต้องครอบคลุม policy changes แต่ไม่ทดสอบ secret จริง',
+  },
+};
+
+export function createCoreLessonContent(summary: LessonSummary, level: Level): LessonContent {
+  const blueprint = coreBlueprints[summary.id as CoreLessonId];
+  const notes = level === 'diploma' ? diplomaNotes[summary.id as CoreLessonId] : undefined;
+  const sections: LessonSection[] = blueprint.sections.map((section) => ({
+    ...section,
+    diplomaNote: notes?.[section.id],
+  }));
+
+  return { ...summary, ...blueprint, sections };
+}
+
+export function createCertificateLessonContent(summaries: LessonSummary[]): LessonContent[] {
+  return summaries.map((summary) => createCoreLessonContent(summary, 'certificate'));
+}

+import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import * as course from '../course';
+import type { LessonId, LessonSummary, Level } from '../../types/course';
+
+type CodeLanguage = 'bash' | 'sql' | 'javascript' | 'vue' | 'json' | 'text';
+
+interface CodeSnippet {
+  id: string;
+  filename: string;
+  language: CodeLanguage;
+  code: string;
+  notes: string[];
+  caption?: string;
+}
+
+interface LessonContent extends LessonSummary {
+  intro: string;
+  sections: Array<{
+    id: string;
+    title: string;
+    body: string[];
+    bullets?: string[];
+    snippets?: CodeSnippet[];
+    callouts?: Array<{ tone: 'info' | 'warning' | 'success'; title: string; text: string }>;
+    diplomaNote?: string;
+  }>;
+  checkpoint: { summary: string; checklist: string[] };
+}
+
+interface ContentCourse {
+  getLessonContent: (level: Level, id: LessonId) => LessonContent | undefined;
+  certificateLessonContent: LessonContent[];
+  diplomaLessonContent: LessonContent[];
+}
+
+const contentCourse = course as unknown as ContentCourse;
+const certificateIds: LessonId[] = [
+  'overview',
+  'installation',
+  'setup',
+  'database',
+  'backend',
+  'api',
+  'frontend',
+  'testing',
+];
+const diplomaIds: LessonId[] = [...certificateIds, 'security', 'devops'];
+const placeholderPattern = /\b(TBD|lorem ipsum|content coming soon|กำลังเตรียมเนื้อหา)\b/i;
+const thaiPattern = /[\u0E00-\u0E7F]/;
+
+function allSnippets(lessons: LessonContent[]) {
+  return lessons.flatMap((lesson) => lesson.sections.flatMap((section) => section.snippets ?? []));
+}
+
+function getSnippet(id: string) {
+  const snippet = allSnippets(contentCourse.diplomaLessonContent).find((item) => item.id === id);
+  expect(snippet, `missing snippet ${id}`).toBeDefined();
+  return snippet!;
+}
+
+function lessonText(lesson: LessonContent) {
+  return [
+    lesson.intro,
+    ...lesson.sections.flatMap((section) => [
+      section.title,
+      ...section.body,
+      ...(section.bullets ?? []),
+      ...(section.snippets ?? []).flatMap((snippet) => [snippet.code, snippet.caption ?? '']),
+      ...(section.callouts ?? []).map((callout) => `${callout.title} ${callout.text}`),
+      section.diplomaNote ?? '',
+    ]),
+    lesson.checkpoint.summary,
+    ...lesson.checkpoint.checklist,
+  ].join('\n');
+}
+
+const requiredCoverage: Record<LessonId, string[]> = {
+  overview: ['Register', 'Login', 'Session', 'Frontend', 'Backend', 'Database', 'HTTP', 'JSON'],
+  installation: ['Node.js', 'npm', 'MySQL', 'MariaDB', 'utf8mb4', 'HeidiSQL', 'VS Code', 'Git', 'node --version', 'npm --version'],
+  setup: ['auth-backend', 'auth-frontend', 'npm create vite@latest auth-frontend -- --template vue-ts', 'express', 'mysql2', 'bcryptjs', 'jsonwebtoken', 'cors', 'dotenv', 'express-validator', 'helmet', 'express-rate-limit', 'nodemon', '.env', '.gitignore', 'app.js', 'server.js', 'proxy'],
+  database: ['CREATE DATABASE', 'authlab', 'users', 'PRIMARY KEY', 'UNIQUE', 'utf8mb4', 'password_hash', 'created_at', 'updated_at', 'SQL injection'],
+  backend: ['createPool', 'express-validator', 'bcrypt.hash', 'bcrypt.compare', 'ER_DUP_ENTRY', '/me', '401', '409', '422'],
+  api: ['jwt.sign', 'jwt.verify', 'Bearer', '/api/auth/register', '/api/auth/login', '/api/auth/me', 'cors', 'rateLimit', 'curl', 'Network'],
+  frontend: ['Composition API', 'axios.create', 'defineStore', 'beforeEach', 'LoginView', 'RegisterView', 'DashboardView', 'loading', 'error', 'autocomplete', 'logout', 'localStorage'],
+  testing: ['duplicate', 'weak', 'wrong password', 'missing token', 'expired token', 'curl', 'Thunder Client', 'Vitest', 'isPasswordStrongEnough'],
+  security: ['JWT', 'Session', 'rotation', 'revocation', 'HttpOnly', 'cookie', 'XSS', 'CSRF', 'CORS', 'rate limit', 'enumeration', 'secret'],
+  devops: ['Dockerfile', '.dockerignore', 'docker-compose.yaml', 'healthcheck', 'volumes', 'docker compose', 'test', 'build', 'push', 'deploy', 'secrets'],
+};
+
+describe('AuthLab lesson content', () => {
+  it('exposes the complete eight- and ten-lesson registries', () => {
+    expect(contentCourse.getLessonContent).toBeTypeOf('function');
+    expect(contentCourse.certificateLessonContent.map((lesson) => lesson.id)).toEqual(certificateIds);
+    expect(contentCourse.diplomaLessonContent.map((lesson) => lesson.id)).toEqual(diplomaIds);
+
+    for (const [level, ids] of [['certificate', certificateIds], ['diploma', diplomaIds]] as const) {
+      for (const id of ids) {
+        const content = contentCourse.getLessonContent(level, id);
+        const summary = course.getLessonSummary(level, id);
+        expect(content, `${level}/${id}`).toBeDefined();
+        expect(content).toMatchObject({ ...summary, id, level });
+      }
+    }
+
+    expect(contentCourse.getLessonContent('certificate', 'security')).toBeUndefined();
+    expect(contentCourse.getLessonContent('certificate', 'devops')).toBeUndefined();
+  });
+
+  it.each([
+    ['certificate', certificateIds],
+    ['diploma', diplomaIds],
+  ] as const)('provides substantive, unique %s lesson sections and checkpoints', (level, ids) => {
+    const lessons = level === 'certificate'
+      ? contentCourse.certificateLessonContent
+      : contentCourse.diplomaLessonContent;
+
+    for (const id of ids) {
+      const lesson = contentCourse.getLessonContent(level, id)!;
+      expect(lesson.intro.length, `${id} intro`).toBeGreaterThan(80);
+      expect(thaiPattern.test(lesson.intro), `${id} Thai intro`).toBe(true);
+      expect(lesson.sections.length, `${id} sections`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);
+      expect(new Set(lesson.sections.map((section) => section.id)).size).toBe(lesson.sections.length);
+      expect(lesson.checkpoint.checklist.length, `${id} checkpoint`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);
+      expect(lesson.checkpoint.summary.length).toBeGreaterThan(40);
+
+      for (const section of lesson.sections) {
+        expect(section.title.length, `${id}/${section.id} title`).toBeGreaterThan(3);
+        expect(section.body.length, `${id}/${section.id} body`).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+        expect(section.body.join(' ').length, `${id}/${section.id} substance`).toBeGreaterThan(100);
+      }
+    }
+
+    expect(lessons).toHaveLength(ids.length);
+  });
+
+  it.each([
+    ['certificate', certificateIds],
+    ['diploma', diplomaIds],
+  ] as const)('gives every %s code line a distinct Thai explanation', (level, ids) => {
+    const snippets = allSnippets(
+      ids.map((id) => contentCourse.getLessonContent(level, id)!),
+    );
+
+    for (const lessonId of ids) {
+      const lesson = contentCourse.getLessonContent(level, lessonId)!;
+      const lessonSnippets = allSnippets([lesson]);
+      expect(lessonSnippets.length, `${lessonId} snippets`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
+      expect(lessonSnippets.reduce((total, snippet) => total + snippet.code.split('\n').length, 0), `${lessonId} code depth`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(12);
+    }
+
+    for (const snippet of snippets) {
+      const lines = snippet.code.split('\n');
+      expect(snippet.id).toMatch(/^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)+$/);
+      expect(snippet.filename).not.toMatch(/\.\./);
+      expect(['bash', 'sql', 'javascript', 'vue', 'json', 'text']).toContain(snippet.language);
+      expect(snippet.notes, `${snippet.id} note count`).toHaveLength(lines.length);
+      expect(new Set(snippet.notes).size, `${snippet.id} repeated notes`).toBe(lines.length);
+      snippet.notes.forEach((note, index) => {
+        expect(note.trim().length, `${snippet.id} line ${index + 1}`).toBeGreaterThan(8);
+        expect(thaiPattern.test(note), `${snippet.id} line ${index + 1} Thai`).toBe(true);
+      });
+    }
+
+    const diplomaSnippets = allSnippets(contentCourse.diplomaLessonContent);
+    expect(new Set(diplomaSnippets.map((snippet) => snippet.id)).size).toBe(diplomaSnippets.length);
+  });
+
+  it.each(diplomaIds)('covers the required %s curriculum without placeholder copy', (id) => {
+    const lesson = contentCourse.getLessonContent('diploma', id)!;
+    const text = lessonText(lesson);
+    expect(text).not.toMatch(placeholderPattern);
+    for (const term of requiredCoverage[id]) {
+      expect(text, `${id} missing ${term}`).toContain(term);
+    }
+  });
+
+  it('adds meaningful diploma trade-offs instead of relabeling the core', () => {
+    for (const id of certificateIds) {
+      const certificate = contentCourse.getLessonContent('certificate', id)!;
+      const diploma = contentCourse.getLessonContent('diploma', id)!;
+      expect(diploma.sections.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(certificate.sections.length);
+      expect(diploma.sections.some((section) => Boolean(section.diplomaNote))).toBe(true);
+      for (const section of diploma.sections) {
+        if (section.diplomaNote) {
+          expect(section.diplomaNote.length).toBeGreaterThan(80);
+          expect(thaiPattern.test(section.diplomaNote)).toBe(true);
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  });
+
+  it('keeps the security model explicit and free of sample production secrets', () => {
+    const securityText = lessonText(contentCourse.getLessonContent('diploma', 'security')!);
+    expect(securityText).toContain('parameterized');
+    expect(securityText).toContain('bcrypt');
+    expect(securityText).toMatch(/generic|ข้อความเดียวกัน/);
+    expect(securityText).toContain('process.env.JWT_SECRET');
+    expect(securityText).toMatch(/localStorage/);
+    expect(securityText).toMatch(/Demo.*ไม่ใช่ production|ไม่ใช่ production auth/i);
+    expect(securityText).not.toMatch(/(supersecret|my-secret|change-me|password123)/i);
+    expect(securityText).not.toMatch(/process\.env\.JWT_SECRET\s*\|\|\s*['"][^'"]+['"]/);
+  });
+

+    const routes = getSnippet('backend-auth-routes').code;
+    expect(routes.indexOf('const router = Router();')).toBeGreaterThan(-1);
+    expect(routes.indexOf('const router = Router();')).toBeLessThan(routes.indexOf("router.post('/register'"));
+

+    expect(setupApp).not.toContain("import authRoutes from './routes/authRoutes.js'");

+    expect(routes).toContain("router.post('/logout', requireTrustedOrigin, cookieParser()");
+    expect(routes).toContain('CLIENT_ORIGINS');
+

+    expect(service).toContain('beginTransaction()');

+    expect(service).toContain('commit()');
+    expect(service).toContain('rollback()');
+    expect(getSnippet('security-refresh-token-service').notes).toContain(

+    );
+  });
+
+  it('deploys the same immutable images that CI pushes and includes the nginx config', () => {
+    const dockerfile = getSnippet('devops-frontend-dockerfile').code;
+    expect(dockerfile).toContain('COPY nginx.conf ./nginx.conf');
+
+    const compose = getSnippet('devops-compose').code;
+    expect(compose).toContain('./database/schema.sql:/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/01-schema.sql:ro');
+    expect(compose).toContain('image: ${AUTH_BACKEND_IMAGE:-authlab-backend:local}');

+    expect(nginxNotes[nginxLines.indexOf('    add_header Referrer-Policy strict-origin-when-cross-origin always;')]).toContain('Referrer-Policy');
+  });
+
+  it('creates an expired token for an existing user so expiry is the only rejection cause', () => {
+    const script = getSnippet('testing-create-expired-token').code;
+    expect(script).toContain('SELECT id FROM users ORDER BY id LIMIT 1');
+    expect(script).toContain('sub: process.argv[1]');
+    expect(script).toContain("algorithm: 'HS256'");
+    expect(getSnippet('testing-create-expired-token').caption).toContain('Register');
+  });
+

+    const diplomaText = lessonText(contentCourse.getLessonContent('diploma', 'setup')!);
+    expect(diplomaText).toContain('authlab');
+    expect(diplomaText).toContain('password_hash');
+    expect(diplomaText).toContain('/api/auth/me');
+    expect(diplomaText).toMatch(/ผลลัพธ์|คาดว่า|ตรวจสอบ/);
+
+    for (const id of ['installation', 'setup', 'testing', 'devops'] as const) {
+      const text = lessonText(contentCourse.getLessonContent('diploma', id)!);
+      expect(text, `${id} verification`).toMatch(/ผลลัพธ์|คาดว่า|ตรวจสอบ/);
+    }
+  });
+});

+import type { LessonContent, LessonSummary } from '../../types/course';
+import { devopsSnippets } from '../snippets/devops';
+import { securitySnippets } from '../snippets/security';
+import { createCoreLessonContent } from './certificate';
+
+type AdvancedLessonId = 'security' | 'devops';
+type AdvancedBlueprint = Omit<LessonContent, keyof LessonSummary>;
+
+const advancedBlueprints: Record<AdvancedLessonId, AdvancedBlueprint> = {
+  security: {
+    intro:
+      'ยกระดับความเข้าใจด้าน authentication จาก JWT ขั้นพื้นฐานไปสู่ lifecycle, rotation, revocation, HttpOnly refresh cookie และ threat model สำหรับ XSS, CSRF, CORS, rate limit, user enumeration และ production secret management',
+    sections: [
+      {
+        id: 'jwt-vs-session',
+        title: 'JWT กับ Server-side Session: เลือกโมเดลที่เหมาะกับข้อจำกัด',
+        body: [

+    },
+  },
+};
+
+export function createDiplomaLessonContent(summaries: LessonSummary[]): LessonContent[] {
+  return summaries.map((summary) => {
+    if (summary.id === 'security' || summary.id === 'devops') {
+      return { ...summary, ...advancedBlueprints[summary.id] };
+    }
+    return createCoreLessonContent(summary, 'diploma');
+  });
+}

+import { defineSnippet } from './defineSnippet';
+
+export const backendSnippets = [
+  defineSnippet(
+    'backend-mysql-pool',
+    'auth-backend/src/db/pool.js',
+    'javascript',
+    `import 'dotenv/config';
+import mysql from 'mysql2/promise';
+
+const pool = mysql.createPool({
+  host: process.env.DB_HOST ?? 'localhost',
+  port: Number(process.env.DB_PORT ?? 3306),
+  user: process.env.DB_USER,
+  password: process.env.DB_PASSWORD,
+  database: process.env.DB_NAME ?? 'authlab',
+  waitForConnections: true,
+  connectionLimit: 10,
+  queueLimit: 0,
+  charset: 'UTF8MB4_GENERAL_CI',
+});
+
+export default pool;`,
+    [
+      'โหลด environment variables ก่อนสร้างการเชื่อมต่อฐานข้อมูล',
+      'นำเข้า mysql2/promise เพื่อใช้ async/await กับ query ทั้งหมด',
+      'เว้นบรรทัดเพื่อแยกส่วนนำเข้า module ออกจากการสร้าง pool ให้อ่านง่าย',
+      'สร้าง pool ที่แชร์ connection ระหว่าง request แทนการเปิดใหม่ทุกครั้ง',

+  defineSnippet(
+    'backend-password-policy',
+    'auth-backend/src/utils/passwordPolicy.js',
+    'javascript',
+    `export function isPasswordStrongEnough(value) {
+  if (typeof value !== 'string') return false;
+  if (value.length < 8 || value.length > 72) return false;
+  if (Buffer.byteLength(value, 'utf8') > 72) return false;
+  if (!/[a-z]/.test(value)) return false;
+  if (!/[A-Z]/.test(value)) return false;
+  if (!/[0-9]/.test(value)) return false;
+  if (!/[^A-Za-z0-9]/.test(value)) return false;
+  return true;
+}
+
+export const passwordPolicyMessage =
+  'รหัสผ่านต้องมี 8–72 ตัว อักษรพิมพ์เล็ก พิมพ์ใหญ่ ตัวเลข และอักขระพิเศษ';`,

+  defineSnippet(
+    'backend-access-token',
+    'auth-backend/src/utils/token.js',
+    'javascript',
+    `import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';
+
+export function getJwtSecret() {
+  const secret = process.env.JWT_SECRET;
+  if (!secret || secret.length < 32) {
+    throw new Error('JWT_SECRET ต้องมีความยาวอย่างน้อย 32 ตัวอักษร');
+  }
+  return secret;
+}
+
+export function signAccessToken(user) {
+  return jwt.sign(
+    { sub: String(user.id) },
+    getJwtSecret(),
+    { expiresIn: process.env.JWT_EXPIRES_IN ?? '15m', algorithm: 'HS256', issuer: 'authlab', audience: 'authlab-client' },
+  );
+}`,

+  ),
+  defineSnippet(
+    'backend-auth-middleware',
+    'auth-backend/src/middleware/auth.js',
+    'javascript',
+    `import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';
+import { getJwtSecret } from '../utils/token.js';
+
+export function requireAuth(req, res, next) {
+  const authorization = req.get('authorization') ?? '';
+  const [scheme, token] = authorization.split(' ');
+
+  if (scheme !== 'Bearer' || !token) {
+    return res.status(401).json({ status: 'error', message: 'ต้องเข้าสู่ระบบก่อน', data: null });
+  }
+
+  try {
+    const payload = jwt.verify(token, getJwtSecret(), {
+      algorithms: ['HS256'],
+      issuer: 'authlab',
+      audience: 'authlab-client',
+    });
+    req.user = { id: Number(payload.sub) };
+    return next();
+  } catch {
+    return res.status(401).json({ status: 'error', message: 'ต้องเข้าสู่ระบบก่อน', data: null });
+  }
+}`,
+    [
+      'นำเข้า jwt.verify สำหรับตรวจลายเซ็นและอายุของ access token',
+      'นำเข้า getJwtSecret ที่ปิดกัดการใช้งาน secret ที่ว่างหรืออ่อน',
+      'เว้นบรรทัดเพื่อแยก dependency ออกจาก middleware',
+      'ส่งออก middleware ที่บังคับ auth ก่อนให้ route ทำงาน',
+      'อ่าน Authorization header และใช้ string ว่างเมื่อ client ไม่ส่งมา',
+      'แยก scheme กับ token ด้วยช่องว่างตามรูปแบบ Bearer <token>',

+      'เริ่มเงื่อนไขตรวจ scheme และการมีอยู่ของ token',
+      'ปฏิเสธ header ที่ไม่ใช่ Bearer หรือไม่มี token ด้วยสถานะ 401',

+      'ปิด if หลังคืน response เพื่อไม่รัน verification ต่อ',
+      'เว้นบรรทัดเพื่อแยก input validation ออกจาก cryptographic verification',
+      'เปิด jwt.verify พร้อม secret และ options ที่จำกัดการยืนยัน',
+      'อนุญาตเฉพาะ algorithm HS256 เพื่อไม่ยอมรับวิธีอื่นที่ไม่ได้กำหนด',

+      'กำหนด audience authlab-client เพื่อแยก token สำหรับ client นี้',
+      'ปิด options ของ jwt.verify',
+      'อ่าน subject จาก verified payload และแปลงเป็น id ตัวเลข',
+      'แนบ user id เข้า req เพื่อ controller ใช้โดยไม่เชื่อม userId จาก body',
+      'อนุญาตให้ request ผ่านไปยัง route ถัดไปเมื่อ verify สำเร็จ',
+      'จับ error ทั้งหมดสำหรับ token ที่ผิด หมดอายุหรือ config ไม่ถูกต้อง',
+      'ตอบ 401 ด้วยข้อความทั่วไปเพื่อไม่เปิดเผยรายละเอียดการยืนยัน',
+      'ปิด catch เพื่อสิ้นสุด middleware และปิดฟังก์ชัน',
+    ],
+    'JWT ไม่ได้ encrypt ข้อมูล; ห้ามเก็บ password หรือ secret ใน payload',
+  ),
+  defineSnippet(
+    'backend-auth-controller',
+    'auth-backend/src/controllers/authController.js',
+    'javascript',
+    `import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';
+import { validationResult } from 'express-validator';
+import { createUser, findByEmail, findById } from '../models/userModel.js';
+import { signAccessToken } from '../utils/token.js';
+
+function publicUser(user) {
+  return { id: user.id, email: user.email, created_at: user.created_at ?? null };
+}
+
+export async function register(req, res, next) {
+  const errors = validationResult(req);
+  if (!errors.isEmpty()) {
+    return res.status(422).json({
+      status: 'error',
+      message: 'ข้อมูลที่กรอกไม่ถูกต้อง',
+      data: { errors: errors.array().map((error) => ({ field: error.path, message: error.msg })) },
+    });
+  }
+
+  const email = req.body.email.trim().toLowerCase();
+  const passwordHash = await bcrypt.hash(req.body.password, 12);
+
+  try {
+    const existing = await findByEmail(email);
+    if (existing) {
+      return res.status(409).json({ status: 'error', message: 'อีเมลนี้ถูกใช้งานแล้ว', data: null });
+    }
+    const id = await createUser(email, passwordHash);
+    const token = signAccessToken({ id });
+    return res.status(201).json({
+      status: 'success',
+      message: 'สมัครสมาชิกสำเร็จ',
+      data: { token, user: publicUser({ id, email }) },
+    });
+  } catch (error) {
+    if (error.code === 'ER_DUP_ENTRY') {
+      return res.status(409).json({ status: 'error', message: 'อีเมลนี้ถูกใช้งานแล้ว', data: null });
+    }
+    return next(error);
+  }
+}
+
+export async function login(req, res, next) {
+  const errors = validationResult(req);
+  if (!errors.isEmpty()) {
+    return res.status(422).json({ status: 'error', message: 'ข้อมูลที่กรอกไม่ถูกต้อง', data: null });
+  }
+
+  const email = req.body.email.trim().toLowerCase();
+  const user = await findByEmail(email);
+  const validPassword = user ? await bcrypt.compare(req.body.password, user.password_hash) : false;
+
+  if (!user || !validPassword) {
+    return res.status(401).json({ status: 'error', message: 'อีเมลหรือรหัสผ่านไม่ถูกต้อง', data: null });
+  }
+
+  const token = signAccessToken({ id: user.id });
+  return res.status(200).json({
+    status: 'success',
+    message: 'เข้าสู่ระบบสำเร็จ',
+    data: { token, user: publicUser(user) },
+  });
+}
+
+export async function me(req, res, next) {
+  const user = await findById(req.user.id);
+  if (!user) {
+    return res.status(401).json({ status: 'error', message: 'ไม่พบผู้ใช้ที่เข้าสู่ระบบ', data: null });
+  }
+  return res.status(200).json({ status: 'success', message: 'อ่านข้อมูลผู้ใช้สำเร็จ', data: { user } });
+}`,

diff --git a/src/data/snippets/defineSnippet.ts b/src/data/snippets/defineSnippet.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..e98a594
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/data/snippets/defineSnippet.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,16 @@
+import type { CodeLanguage, CodeSnippet } from '../../types/course';
+
+export function defineSnippet(
+  id: string,
+  filename: string,
+  language: CodeLanguage,
+  code: string,
+  notes: string[],
+  caption?: string,
+): CodeSnippet {
+  const lineCount = code.split('\n').length;
+  if (notes.length !== lineCount) {
+    throw new Error(`Snippet ${id} has ${lineCount} code lines but ${notes.length} notes.`);
+  }
+  return { id, filename, language, code, notes, caption };
+}

+    - if: '$CI_COMMIT_BRANCH == "main"'

+const api = axios.create({
+  baseURL: import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL || '/api',
+  headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
+});
+
+api.interceptors.request.use((config) => {
+  const token = localStorage.getItem('authlab.token');
+  if (token) config.headers.Authorization = 'Bearer ' + token;
+  return config;
+});
+
+api.interceptors.response.use(
+  (response) => response,
+  (error) => {
+    const message = error.response?.data?.message || 'ไม่สามารถเชื่อมต่อเซิร์ฟเวอร์ได้';
+    const requestError = new Error(message);
+    requestError.status = error.response?.status;
+    return Promise.reject(requestError);
+  },
+);
+
+export default api;`,

+  defineSnippet(
+    'frontend-auth-store',
+    'auth-frontend/src/stores/auth.js',
+    'javascript',
+    `import { computed, ref } from 'vue';
+import { defineStore } from 'pinia';
+import api from '../api/http.js';
+
+const TOKEN_KEY = 'authlab.token';
+
+export const useAuthStore = defineStore('auth', () => {
+  const token = ref(localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY));
+  const user = ref(null);
+  const loading = ref(false);
+  const error = ref('');
+  const isAuthenticated = computed(() => Boolean(token.value));
+
+  async function authenticate(endpoint, payload) {
+    loading.value = true;
+    error.value = '';
+    try {
+      const response = await api.post('/auth/' + endpoint, payload);
+      token.value = response.data.data.token;
+      user.value = response.data.data.user;
+      localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, token.value);
+    } catch (cause) {
+      error.value = cause.message;
+      throw cause;
+    } finally {
+      loading.value = false;
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function loadMe() {
+    if (!token.value) return;
+    loading.value = true;
+    error.value = '';
+    try {
+      const response = await api.get('/auth/me');
+      user.value = response.data.data.user;
+    } catch (cause) {
+      error.value = cause.message;
+      if (cause.status === 401) logout();
+    } finally {
+      loading.value = false;
+    }
+  }
+
+  function logout() {
+    token.value = null;
+    user.value = null;
+    localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
+  }
+
+  return { token, user, loading, error, isAuthenticated, authenticate, loadMe, logout };
+});`,
+    [
+      "นำเข้า ref และ computed จาก Vue Composition API สำหรับ state แบบ reactive",

+    'frontend-router-guard',
+    'auth-frontend/src/router/index.js',
+    'javascript',
+    `import { createRouter, createWebHistory } from 'vue-router';
+import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth.js';
+import LoginView from '../views/LoginView.vue';
+import RegisterView from '../views/RegisterView.vue';
+import DashboardView from '../views/DashboardView.vue';
+
+const router = createRouter({
+  history: createWebHistory(),
+  routes: [
+    { path: '/', redirect: '/dashboard' },
+    { path: '/login', component: LoginView, meta: { guestOnly: true } },
+    { path: '/register', component: RegisterView, meta: { guestOnly: true } },
+    { path: '/dashboard', component: DashboardView, meta: { requiresAuth: true } },
+  ],
+});
+
+router.beforeEach((to) => {
+  const auth = useAuthStore();
+  if (to.meta.requiresAuth && !auth.isAuthenticated) return '/login';
+  if (to.meta.guestOnly && auth.isAuthenticated) return '/dashboard';
+  return true;
+});
+
+export default router;`,

+import { reactive, ref } from 'vue';
+import { useRouter } from 'vue-router';
+import { RouterLink } from 'vue-router';
+import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth.js';
+
+const router = useRouter();
+const auth = useAuthStore();
+const submitting = ref(false);
+const error = ref('');
+const form = reactive({ email: '', password: '' });
+
+async function submit() {
+  submitting.value = true;
+  error.value = '';
+  try {
+    await auth.authenticate('login', { email: form.email, password: form.password });
+    await router.push('/dashboard');
+  } catch (cause) {
+    error.value = cause.message;
+  } finally {
+    submitting.value = false;
+  }
+}
+</script>
+
+<template>
+  <section class="mx-auto max-w-md rounded-2xl bg-white p-6 shadow">

+    <form class="mt-6 space-y-4" @submit.prevent="submit">
+      <div>
+        <label for="login-email" class="block text-sm font-medium">อีเมล</label>

+      </div>
+      <div>

+      </div>

+      </button>
+    </form>

+  </section>
+</template>`,

+  defineSnippet(
+    'security-refresh-token-service',
+    'auth-backend/src/services/refreshTokenService.js',
+    'javascript',
+    `import crypto from 'node:crypto';
+import pool from '../db/pool.js';
+
+const REFRESH_DAYS = 7;
+const REFRESH_MAX_AGE = REFRESH_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
+
+function hashToken(token) {
+  return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(token).digest('hex');
+}
+
+export async function issueRefreshToken(res, userId) {
+  const token = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
+  const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + REFRESH_MAX_AGE);
+  await pool.execute(
+    'INSERT INTO refresh_tokens (token_hash, user_id, expires_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)',
+    [hashToken(token), userId, expiresAt],
+  );
+  res.cookie('refresh_token', token, {
+    httpOnly: true,
+    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production',
+    sameSite: 'lax',
+    path: '/api/auth',
+    maxAge: REFRESH_MAX_AGE,
+  });
+}
+
+export async function rotateRefreshToken(rawToken) {
+  if (!rawToken) return null;
+  const connection = await pool.getConnection();
+  try {
+    await connection.beginTransaction();
+    const [rows] = await connection.execute(
+      'SELECT id, user_id, expires_at, revoked_at FROM refresh_tokens WHERE token_hash = ? LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE',
+      [hashToken(rawToken)],
+    );
+    const record = rows[0];
+    if (!record || record.revoked_at || record.expires_at <= new Date()) {
+      await connection.rollback();
+      return null;
+    }
+    const [result] = await connection.execute(
+      'UPDATE refresh_tokens SET revoked_at = NOW() WHERE id = ? AND revoked_at IS NULL AND expires_at > NOW()',
+      [record.id],
+    );
+    if (result.affectedRows !== 1) {
+      await connection.rollback();
+      return null;
+    }
+    await connection.commit();
+    return record.user_id;
+  } catch (error) {
+    await connection.rollback();
+    throw error;
+  } finally {
+    connection.release();
+  }
+}
+
+export async function revokeRefreshToken(rawToken) {
+  if (!rawToken) return;
+  await pool.execute('UPDATE refresh_tokens SET revoked_at = NOW() WHERE token_hash = ? AND revoked_at IS NULL', [hashToken(rawToken)]);
+}`,

+  defineSnippet(
+    'security-refresh-routes',
+    'auth-backend/src/routes/refreshRoutes.js',
+    'javascript',
+    `import { Router } from 'express';
+import cookieParser from 'cookie-parser';
+import { signAccessToken } from '../utils/token.js';
+import { issueRefreshToken, revokeRefreshToken, rotateRefreshToken } from '../services/refreshTokenService.js';
+
+const router = Router();
+const cookieOptions = { httpOnly: true, secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production', sameSite: 'lax', path: '/api/auth' };
+
+function requireTrustedOrigin(req, res, next) {
+  const origin = req.get('origin');
+  const allowedOrigins = (process.env.CLIENT_ORIGINS ?? '').split(',').map((item) => item.trim());
+  if (origin && !allowedOrigins.includes(origin)) {
+    return res.status(403).json({ status: 'error', message: 'Origin ไม่อนุญาต', data: null });
+  }
+  return next();
+}
+
+router.post('/refresh', requireTrustedOrigin, cookieParser(), async (req, res, next) => {
+  try {
+    const userId = await rotateRefreshToken(req.cookies.refresh_token);
+    if (!userId) {
+      res.clearCookie('refresh_token', cookieOptions);
+      return res.status(401).json({ status: 'error', message: 'ต้องเข้าสู่ระบบใหม่', data: null });
+    }
+    await issueRefreshToken(res, userId);
+    const token = signAccessToken({ id: userId });
+    return res.status(200).json({ status: 'success', message: 'ต่ออายุ access token สำเร็จ', data: { token } });
+  } catch (error) {
+    return next(error);
+  }
+});
+
+router.post('/logout', requireTrustedOrigin, cookieParser(), async (req, res, next) => {
+  try {
+    await revokeRefreshToken(req.cookies.refresh_token);
+    res.clearCookie('refresh_token', cookieOptions);
+    return res.status(200).json({ status: 'success', message: 'ออกจากระบบสำเร็จ', data: null });
+  } catch (error) {
+    return next(error);
+  }
+});
+
+export default router;`,
+    [
+      "นำเข้า Router สำหรับแยก refresh และ logout routes",

+      "นำเข้า signAccessToken เพื่อออก access token สั้นหลัง rotate สำเร็จ",
+      "นำเข้า service ที่ดูแลการออก หมุน และ revoke refresh token",
+      "เว้นบรรทัดก่อนสร้าง router",
+      "สร้าง router instance สำหรับเมานต์ใต้ /api/auth",
+      "กำหนด cookie options เหมือนตอนออก token รวม HttpOnly, Secure, SameSite และ path เพื่อ clearCookie ตรงกัน",
+      "เว้นบรรทัดก่อน middleware ตรวจ Origin",
+      "ประกาศ middleware สำหรับคำขอที่เปลี่ยนสถานะโดยใช้ cookie",
+      "อ่าน Origin header ที่ browser แนบมา",
+      "แยก CLIENT_ORIGINS เป็น allowlist และ trim ค่าแต่ละ origin",
+      "ปฏิเสธ Origin ที่ส่งมาและไม่อยู่ใน allowlist",
+      "ตอบ 403 ก่อนอ่านหรือเปลี่ยน refresh token เมื่อ Origin ไม่เชื่อถือ",
+      "ปิด if ของ Origin ที่ไม่อนุญาต",
+      "อนุญาตให้ไปต่อเมื่อไม่มี Origin หรือ Origin ตรง allowlist",
+      "ปิด requireTrustedOrigin middleware",
