+      "เปิด POST /refresh โดยตรวจ Origin และอ่าน cookie ก่อน handler",
+      "เริ่ม try block สำหรับ database errors ของ rotation",
+      "อ่าน cookie แล้วตรวจ expiry/revocation และ revoke token เก่าแบบ atomic",
+      "ตรวจ userId ที่ service คืนมาเมื่อ refresh token ใช้ไม่ได้",
+      "ล้าง cookie ที่ไม่สามารถใช้งานต่อได้",
+      "ส่ง generic message ให้ client กลับไป Login",
+      "ปิด if ของ refresh token ที่ไม่ถูกต้อง",
+      "ออก refresh token ใหม่และตั้ง cookie หลัง rotate สำเร็จ",

+  it.each(['short1!', 'lowercase1!', 'NoNumbers!', 'NoSymbol1'])('rejects weak input %s', (password) => {

+  });

+  it('rejects non-string input', () => {

+curl -i -X POST "$BASE_URL/api/auth/login" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d "{\"email\":\"$EMAIL\",\"password\":\"$PASSWORD\"}"
+TOKEN='<นำ access token จาก Login ที่สำเร็จ>'
+curl -i "$BASE_URL/api/auth/me" -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN"
+curl -i "$BASE_URL/api/auth/me"`,
+    [
+      'กำหนด URL พื้นฐานของ Backend ที่กำลังทดสอบ',
+      'สร้าง email ใหม่ด้วย timestamp เพื่อไม่ชน unique constraint เมื่อรันซ้ำ',
+      'เก็บ password ตัวอย่างที่ผ่าน policy ในตัวแปรของ shell',

+      'เรียก /me พร้อม Bearer token เพื่อคาดหวัง 200 และ public user',
+      'เรียก /me โดยไม่มี token เพื่อคาดหวัง 401 จาก middleware',

+  ),
+];

+/// <reference types="node" />
+import { webcrypto } from 'node:crypto';
+import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+import {
+  hashPassword,
+  loginUser,
+  logout,
+  readSession,
+  registerUser,
+  resetDemoData,
+  verifyPassword,
+} from './authDemo';
+
+const USERS_KEY = 'auth-classroom:demo-users:v1';
+const SESSION_KEY = 'auth-classroom:demo-session:v1';
+const REGISTRATION_LOCK = 'auth-classroom:demo-register';
+const validInput = {
+  name: 'นักศึกษา AuthLab',
+  email: 'Student@Example.com',
+  password: 'StudyPass1!',
+};
+
+let originalLocks: PropertyDescriptor | undefined;
+let lockRequest: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>;
+
+beforeEach(() => {
+  vi.stubGlobal('crypto', webcrypto);
+  window.localStorage.clear();
+  window.sessionStorage.clear();
+  originalLocks = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(navigator, 'locks');
+  lockRequest = vi.fn(async (_name: string, callback: () => unknown) => callback());
+  Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'locks', {
+    configurable: true,
+    value: { request: lockRequest },
+  });
+});
+
+afterEach(() => {
+  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
+  if (originalLocks) {
+    Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'locks', originalLocks);
+  } else {
+    Reflect.deleteProperty(navigator, 'locks');
+  }
+});
+
+describe('browser password hashing', () => {
+  it('derives a stable PBKDF2 hash for the same password and salt', async () => {
+    const first = await hashPassword('StudyPass1!', 'fixed-test-salt');
+    const second = await hashPassword('StudyPass1!', 'fixed-test-salt');
+
+    expect(first).toBe(second);
+    expect(first).toMatch(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/);
+    expect(first).not.toContain('StudyPass1!');
+    expect(await verifyPassword('StudyPass1!', { salt: 'fixed-test-salt', passwordHash: first })).toBe(true);
+    expect(await verifyPassword('WrongPass9!', { salt: 'fixed-test-salt', passwordHash: first })).toBe(false);
+  });
+
+  it('uses a random salt for each registered user', async () => {
+    await registerUser(validInput);
+    await registerUser({ ...validInput, email: 'second@example.com' });
+
+    const users = JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem(USERS_KEY) ?? '[]') as Array<{ salt: string }>;
+    expect(users).toHaveLength(2);
+    expect(users[0].salt).not.toBe(users[1].salt);
+  });
+});
+
+describe('demo registration and login', () => {
+  it('normalizes input, stores only a salted hash, and creates a public session', async () => {
+    const session = await registerUser({ ...validInput, name: '  นักศึกษา   AuthLab  ' });
+    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(USERS_KEY) ?? '';
+    const users = JSON.parse(raw) as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
+
+    expect(users).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(users[0]).toMatchObject({
+      name: 'นักศึกษา AuthLab',
+      email: 'student@example.com',
+      createdAt: expect.any(String),
+      salt: expect.any(String),
+      passwordHash: expect.stringMatching(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/),
+    });
+    expect(users[0]).not.toHaveProperty('password');
+    expect(raw).not.toContain(validInput.password);
+    expect(session).toEqual({
+      id: users[0].id,
+      name: 'นักศึกษา AuthLab',
+      email: 'student@example.com',
+      createdAt: users[0].createdAt,
+    });
+    expect(readSession()).toEqual(session);
+  });
+
+  it('rejects a duplicate email case-insensitively', async () => {
+    await registerUser(validInput);
+
+    await expect(registerUser({ ...validInput, email: 'STUDENT@example.com' })).rejects.toThrow(
+      'อีเมลนี้ถูกใช้งานแล้ว',
+    );
+    expect(JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem(USERS_KEY) ?? '[]')).toHaveLength(1);
+  });
+
+  it('serializes the storage write through a named Web Lock', async () => {
+    await registerUser(validInput);
+
+    expect(lockRequest).toHaveBeenCalledWith(REGISTRATION_LOCK, expect.any(Function));
+  });
+
+  it('keeps both accounts when two registrations share the lock', async () => {
+    let activeLocks = 0;
+    let maximumActiveLocks = 0;
+    let lockQueue = Promise.resolve();
+    lockRequest.mockImplementation((_name: string, callback: () => unknown) => {
+      const result = lockQueue.then(async () => {
+        activeLocks += 1;
+        maximumActiveLocks = Math.max(maximumActiveLocks, activeLocks);
+        try {
+          await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 5));
+          return await callback();
+        } finally {
+          activeLocks -= 1;
+        }
+      });
+      lockQueue = result.then(() => undefined, () => undefined);
+      return result;
+    });
+
+    await Promise.all([
+      registerUser(validInput),
+      registerUser({ ...validInput, email: 'second@example.com' }),
+    ]);
+
+    expect(maximumActiveLocks).toBe(1);
+    expect(JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem(USERS_KEY) ?? '[]')).toHaveLength(2);
+  });
+
+  it('explains that registration needs Web Locks when the API is unavailable', async () => {
+    Reflect.deleteProperty(navigator, 'locks');
+
+    await expect(registerUser(validInput)).rejects.toThrow(
+      'เบราว์เซอร์นี้ไม่รองรับ Web Locks สำหรับ Demo กรุณาใช้ HTTPS หรือ localhost',
+    );
+    expect(window.localStorage.getItem(USERS_KEY)).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it.each([
+    [{ ...validInput, name: ' ' }, 'กรุณาใส่ชื่อที่แสดง'],
+    [{ ...validInput, email: 'not-an-email' }, 'กรุณาใส่อีเมลที่ถูกต้อง'],
+    [{ ...validInput, password: 'short1!' }, 'รหัสผ่านต้องมี 8–72 ตัว'],
+    [{ ...validInput, password: 'lowercase1!' }, 'รหัสผ่านต้องมี 8–72 ตัว'],
+    [{ ...validInput, password: 'NoNumbers!' }, 'รหัสผ่านต้องมี 8–72 ตัว'],
+    [{ ...validInput, password: 'NoSymbol1' }, 'รหัสผ่านต้องมี 8–72 ตัว'],
+  ])('rejects invalid registration input', async (input, message) => {
+    await expect(registerUser(input)).rejects.toThrow(message);
+    expect(window.localStorage.getItem(USERS_KEY)).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('uses one generic error for unknown email and wrong password', async () => {
+    await registerUser(validInput);
+
+    const unknown = await loginUser({ email: 'missing@example.com', password: validInput.password }).catch(
+      (error: Error) => error.message,
+    );
+    const wrong = await loginUser({ email: validInput.email, password: 'WrongPass9!' }).catch(
+      (error: Error) => error.message,
+    );
+
+    expect(unknown).toBe('อีเมลหรือรหัสผ่านไม่ถูกต้อง');
+    expect(wrong).toBe(unknown);
+  });
+
+  it('logs in with a normalized email and replaces the current session', async () => {
+    await registerUser(validInput);
+    logout();
+
+    const session = await loginUser({ email: '  STUDENT@Example.com  ', password: validInput.password });
+
+    expect(session.email).toBe('student@example.com');
+    expect(readSession()).toEqual(session);
+  });
+});
+
+describe('demo session lifecycle', () => {
+  it('removes the session on logout', async () => {
+    await registerUser(validInput);
+    expect(readSession()).not.toBeNull();
+
+    logout();
+
+    expect(window.sessionStorage.getItem(SESSION_KEY)).toBeNull();
+    expect(readSession()).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('reports logout failure instead of pretending the session was removed', async () => {
+    await registerUser(validInput);
+    const removeItem = Storage.prototype.removeItem;
+    vi.spyOn(Storage.prototype, 'removeItem').mockImplementation(function (
+      this: Storage,
+      key: string,
+    ) {
+      if (this === window.sessionStorage) {
+        throw new DOMException('Storage blocked', 'SecurityError');
+      }
+      removeItem.call(this, key);
+    });
+
+    expect(() => logout()).toThrow('ไม่สามารถออกจากระบบในเบราว์เซอร์นี้ได้');
+    expect(window.sessionStorage.getItem(SESSION_KEY)).not.toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('discards a stale session when its user no longer exists', async () => {
+    await registerUser(validInput);
+    window.localStorage.setItem(USERS_KEY, '[]');
+
+    expect(readSession()).toBeNull();
+    expect(window.sessionStorage.getItem(SESSION_KEY)).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('recovers from corrupted storage and resets users and session together', async () => {
+    window.localStorage.setItem(USERS_KEY, '{broken');
+    window.sessionStorage.setItem(SESSION_KEY, '{broken');
+
+    expect(readSession()).toBeNull();
+    await expect(registerUser(validInput)).resolves.toMatchObject({ email: 'student@example.com' });
+
+    resetDemoData();
+
+    expect(window.localStorage.getItem(USERS_KEY)).toBeNull();
+    expect(window.sessionStorage.getItem(SESSION_KEY)).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('attempts both reset removals and propagates storage failure', async () => {
+    await registerUser(validInput);
+    const removeItem = Storage.prototype.removeItem;
+    const removeItemSpy = vi
+      .spyOn(Storage.prototype, 'removeItem')
+      .mockImplementation(function (this: Storage, key: string) {
+        if (this === window.localStorage) {
+          throw new DOMException('Storage blocked', 'SecurityError');
+        }
+        removeItem.call(this, key);
+      });
+
+    expect(() => resetDemoData()).toThrow('ไม่สามารถล้างข้อมูล Demo ในเบราว์เซอร์นี้ได้');
+    expect(removeItemSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(SESSION_KEY);
+    expect(window.localStorage.getItem(USERS_KEY)).not.toBeNull();
+  });
+});
diff --git a/src/lib/authDemo.ts b/src/lib/authDemo.ts

@@ -0,0 +1,293 @@
+export const DEMO_USERS_KEY = 'auth-classroom:demo-users:v1';
+export const DEMO_SESSION_KEY = 'auth-classroom:demo-session:v1';
+export const DEMO_REGISTRATION_LOCK = 'auth-classroom:demo-register';
+
+const PBKDF2_ITERATIONS = 120_000;
+
+export interface DemoUser {
+  id: string;
+  name: string;
+  email: string;
+  salt: string;
+  passwordHash: string;
+  createdAt: string;
+}
+
+export interface DemoSession {
+  id: string;
+  name: string;
+  email: string;
+  createdAt: string;
+}
+
+export interface RegisterInput {
+  name: string;
+  email: string;
+  password: string;
+}
+
+export interface LoginInput {
+  email: string;
+  password: string;
+}
+
+export const passwordPolicyMessage =
+  'รหัสผ่านต้องมี 8–72 ตัว อักษรพิมพ์เล็ก พิมพ์ใหญ่ ตัวเลข และอักขระพิเศษ';
+
+function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
+  return typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
+}
+
+function toHex(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
+  return Array.from(bytes, (byte) => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
+}
+
+function createRandomHex(byteLength: number): string {
+  const bytes = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(byteLength));
+  return toHex(bytes);
+}
+
+function normalizeName(value: string): string {
+  return value.trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
+}
+
+function normalizeEmail(value: string): string {
+  return value.trim().toLowerCase();
+}
+
+function isValidEmail(value: string): boolean {
+  return value.length <= 255 && /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(value);
+}
+
+export function getPasswordChecks(password: string) {
+  return [
+    { label: '8–72 ตัว', passed: password.length >= 8 && password.length <= 72 },
+    { label: 'อักษรพิมพ์เล็ก', passed: /[a-z]/.test(password) },
+    { label: 'อักษรพิมพ์ใหญ่', passed: /[A-Z]/.test(password) },
+    { label: 'ตัวเลข', passed: /[0-9]/.test(password) },
+    { label: 'อักขระพิเศษ', passed: /[^A-Za-z0-9]/.test(password) },
+  ];
+}
+
+export function isPasswordStrongEnough(value: string): boolean {
+  return (
+    value.length >= 8 &&
+    value.length <= 72 &&
+    new TextEncoder().encode(value).byteLength <= 72 &&
+    getPasswordChecks(value).every((check) => check.passed)
+  );
+}
+
+export async function hashPassword(password: string, salt: string): Promise<string> {
+  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
+    'raw',
+    new TextEncoder().encode(password),
+    'PBKDF2',
+    false,
+    ['deriveBits'],
+  );
+  const bits = await crypto.subtle.deriveBits(
+    {
+      name: 'PBKDF2',
+      hash: 'SHA-256',
+      salt: new TextEncoder().encode(salt),
+      iterations: PBKDF2_ITERATIONS,
+    },
+    key,
+    256,
+  );
+
+  return toHex(new Uint8Array(bits));
+}
+
+export async function verifyPassword(
+  password: string,
+  stored: Pick<DemoUser, 'salt' | 'passwordHash'>,
+): Promise<boolean> {
+  try {
+    const hash = await hashPassword(password, stored.salt);
+    return hash === stored.passwordHash;
+  } catch {
+    return false;
+  }
+}
+
+function isDemoUser(value: unknown): value is DemoUser {
+  return (
+    isRecord(value) &&
+    typeof value.id === 'string' &&
+    typeof value.name === 'string' &&
+    typeof value.email === 'string' &&
+    typeof value.salt === 'string' &&
+    typeof value.passwordHash === 'string' &&
+    typeof value.createdAt === 'string'
+  );
+}
+
+function readUsers(): DemoUser[] {
+  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
+    return [];
+  }
+
+  try {
+    const stored = window.localStorage.getItem(DEMO_USERS_KEY);
+    const parsed: unknown = stored ? JSON.parse(stored) : [];
+    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed.filter(isDemoUser) : [];
+  } catch {
+    return [];
+  }
+}
+
+function writeUsers(users: DemoUser[]): void {
+  try {
+    window.localStorage.setItem(DEMO_USERS_KEY, JSON.stringify(users));
+  } catch {
+    throw new Error('ไม่สามารถบันทึกข้อมูล Demo ในเบราว์เซอร์นี้ได้');
+  }
+}
+
+function toSession(user: DemoUser): DemoSession {
+  return {
+    id: user.id,
+    name: user.name,
+    email: user.email,
+    createdAt: user.createdAt,
+  };
+}
+
+function writeSession(session: DemoSession): void {
+  try {
+    window.sessionStorage.setItem(DEMO_SESSION_KEY, JSON.stringify(session));
+  } catch {
+    throw new Error('ไม่สามารถสร้าง session ในเบราว์เซอร์นี้ได้');
+  }
+}
+
+export async function registerUser(input: RegisterInput): Promise<DemoSession> {
+  const name = normalizeName(input.name);
+  const email = normalizeEmail(input.email);
+
+  if (name.length < 2 || name.length > 80) {
+    throw new Error('กรุณาใส่ชื่อที่แสดง');
+  }
+  if (!isValidEmail(email)) {
+    throw new Error('กรุณาใส่อีเมลที่ถูกต้อง');
+  }
+  if (!isPasswordStrongEnough(input.password)) {
+    throw new Error(passwordPolicyMessage);
+  }
+  if (typeof navigator === 'undefined' || !navigator.locks) {
+    throw new Error(
+      'เบราว์เซอร์นี้ไม่รองรับ Web Locks สำหรับ Demo กรุณาใช้ HTTPS หรือ localhost',
+    );
+  }
+
+  const existingUsers = readUsers();
+  if (existingUsers.some((user) => user.email === email)) {
+    throw new Error('อีเมลนี้ถูกใช้งานแล้ว');
+  }
+
+  const salt = createRandomHex(16);
+  const passwordHash = await hashPassword(input.password, salt);
+  const user: DemoUser = {
+    id: createRandomHex(16),
+    name,
+    email,
+    salt,
+    passwordHash,
+    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
+  };
+
+  await navigator.locks.request(DEMO_REGISTRATION_LOCK, () => {
+    const users = readUsers();
+    if (users.some((candidate) => candidate.email === email)) {
+      throw new Error('อีเมลนี้ถูกใช้งานแล้ว');
+    }
+    writeUsers([...users, user]);
+  });
+
+  const session = toSession(user);
+  writeSession(session);
+  return session;
+}
+
+export async function loginUser(input: LoginInput): Promise<DemoSession> {
+  const email = normalizeEmail(input.email);
+  const user = readUsers().find((candidate) => candidate.email === email);
+  const passwordMatches = user ? await verifyPassword(input.password, user) : false;
+
+  if (!user || !passwordMatches) {
+    throw new Error('อีเมลหรือรหัสผ่านไม่ถูกต้อง');
+  }
+
+  const session = toSession(user);
+  writeSession(session);
+  return session;
+}
+
+export function readSession(): DemoSession | null {
+  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  try {
+    const stored = window.sessionStorage.getItem(DEMO_SESSION_KEY);
+    const parsed: unknown = stored ? JSON.parse(stored) : null;
+    if (
+      !isRecord(parsed) ||
+      typeof parsed.id !== 'string' ||
+      typeof parsed.name !== 'string' ||
+      typeof parsed.email !== 'string' ||
+      typeof parsed.createdAt !== 'string'
+    ) {
+      if (stored) {
+        window.sessionStorage.removeItem(DEMO_SESSION_KEY);
+      }
+      return null;
+    }
+
+    const user = readUsers().find((candidate) => candidate.id === parsed.id);
+    if (!user) {
+      window.sessionStorage.removeItem(DEMO_SESSION_KEY);
+      return null;
+    }
+
+    return toSession(user);
+  } catch {
+    return null;
+  }
+}
+
+export function logout(): void {
+  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
+    return;
+  }
+
+  try {
+    window.sessionStorage.removeItem(DEMO_SESSION_KEY);
+  } catch {
+    throw new Error('ไม่สามารถออกจากระบบในเบราว์เซอร์นี้ได้');
+  }
+}
+
+export function resetDemoData(): void {
+  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
+    return;
+  }
+
+  let failed = false;
+  try {
+    window.localStorage.removeItem(DEMO_USERS_KEY);
+  } catch {
+    failed = true;
+  }
+  try {
+    window.sessionStorage.removeItem(DEMO_SESSION_KEY);
+  } catch {
+    failed = true;
+  }
+
+  if (failed) {
+    throw new Error('ไม่สามารถล้างข้อมูล Demo ในเบราว์เซอร์นี้ได้');
+  }
+}

+import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import { quizzes } from '../data/quizzes';
+import type { Level } from '../types/course';
+import { getQuizQuestions, hasPassed, passingScore, scoreQuiz } from './quiz';
+
+const levels: Level[] = ['certificate', 'diploma'];
+const thaiCharacter = /[\u0E00-\u0E7F]/;
+
+describe('quiz data and helpers', () => {
+  it.each(levels)('provides five valid Thai questions for %s', (level) => {
+    const questions = quizzes[level];
+
+    expect(questions).toHaveLength(5);
+    expect(getQuizQuestions(level)).toEqual(questions);
+    expect(new Set(questions.map((question) => question.id)).size).toBe(5);
+
+    for (const question of questions) {
+      expect(question.id.trim()).not.toBe('');
+      expect(question.prompt).toMatch(thaiCharacter);
+      expect(question.explanation).toMatch(thaiCharacter);
+      expect(question.options).toHaveLength(4);
+      expect(Number.isInteger(question.answer)).toBe(true);
+      expect(question.answer).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
+      expect(question.answer).toBeLessThanOrEqual(3);
+
+      for (const option of question.options) {
+        expect(option.trim()).not.toBe('');
+      }
+    }
+  });
+
+  it('covers the required shared and track-specific concepts', () => {
+    const certificateText = quizzes.certificate
+      .flatMap((question) => [question.prompt, ...question.options, question.explanation])
+      .join(' ');
+    const diplomaText = quizzes.diploma
+      .flatMap((question) => [question.prompt, ...question.options, question.explanation])
+      .join(' ');
+
+    for (const content of [certificateText, diplomaText]) {
+      expect(content).toMatch(/HTTP/);
+      expect(content).toMatch(/GET|POST|PUT|DELETE/);
+      expect(content).toMatch(/200|201|204|404/);
+      expect(content).toMatch(/SQL|MySQL/);
+      expect(content).toMatch(/parameterized|UNIQUE/i);
+      expect(content).toMatch(/validation|ตรวจสอบข้อมูล/i);
+    }
+
+    expect(certificateText).toMatch(/bcrypt/);
+    expect(certificateText).toMatch(/Register|Login|flow|test|ทดสอบ/i);
+    expect(diplomaText).toMatch(/JWT middleware/);
+    expect(diplomaText).toMatch(/Docker/);
+    expect(diplomaText).toMatch(/CI\/CD/);
+    expect(diplomaText).toMatch(/secrets?/i);
+    expect(diplomaText).toMatch(/tests?|ทดสอบ/i);
+  });
+
+  it.each(levels)('scores all correct answers as five for %s', (level) => {
+    const answers = getQuizQuestions(level).map((question) => question.answer);
+
+    expect(scoreQuiz(level, answers)).toBe(5);
+  });
+
+  it('scores mixed certificate answers with the exact expected total', () => {
+    const correctAnswers = quizzes.certificate.map((question) => question.answer);
+    const mixedAnswers = correctAnswers.map((answer, index) =>
+      index === 0 || index === 3 ? (answer + 1) % 4 : answer,
+    );
+
+    expect(scoreQuiz('certificate', mixedAnswers)).toBe(3);
+  });
+
+  it.each(levels)('scores all wrong answers as zero for %s', (level) => {
+    const answers = getQuizQuestions(level).map((question) => (question.answer + 1) % 4);
+
+    expect(scoreQuiz(level, answers)).toBe(0);
+  });
+
+  it.each([
+    { answers: [] },
+    { answers: [0, 1, 2, 3] },
+    { answers: [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 0] },
+  ])('rejects incomplete or oversized answer input %#', ({ answers }) => {
+    expect(() => scoreQuiz('certificate', answers)).toThrowError('คำตอบยังไม่ครบ');
+  });
+
+  it('uses four out of five as the passing score', () => {
+    expect(passingScore).toBe(4);
+    expect(hasPassed(3)).toBe(false);
+    expect(hasPassed(4)).toBe(true);
+    expect(hasPassed(5)).toBe(true);
+  });
+});
diff --git a/src/lib/quiz.ts b/src/lib/quiz.ts

+++ b/src/lib/quiz.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,24 @@
+import { quizzes } from '../data/quizzes';
+import type { Level } from '../types/course';
+
+export const passingScore = 4;
+
+export function getQuizQuestions(level: Level) {
+  return quizzes[level];
+}
+
+export function scoreQuiz(level: Level, answers: readonly number[]): number {
+  if (answers.length !== 5) {
+    throw new Error('คำตอบยังไม่ครบ');
+  }
+
+  const questions = getQuizQuestions(level);
+  return answers.reduce(
+    (score, answer, index) => score + (answer === questions[index].answer ? 1 : 0),
+    0,
+  );
+}
+
+export function hasPassed(score: number): boolean {
+  return score >= passingScore;
+}

+import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+import indexHtml from '../../index.html?raw';
+import {
+  THEME_STORAGE_KEY,
+  applyTheme,
+  readThemePreference,
+  resolveTheme,
+  writeThemePreference,
+} from './theme';
+
+function installMatchMedia(matches: boolean) {
+  Object.defineProperty(window, 'matchMedia', {
+    configurable: true,
+    value: vi.fn(() => ({ matches })),
+  });
+}
+
+function runBootstrap() {
+  const script = indexHtml.match(/<script id="theme-bootstrap">([\s\S]*?)<\/script>/)?.[1];
+  if (!script) {
+    throw new Error('Theme bootstrap script was not found.');
+  }
+  new Function(script)();
+}
+
+beforeEach(() => {
+  localStorage.clear();
+  document.documentElement.removeAttribute('data-theme');
+  document.documentElement.removeAttribute('style');
+  document.head.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]')?.remove();
+  installMatchMedia(false);
+});
+
+afterEach(() => {
+  localStorage.clear();
+  document.documentElement.removeAttribute('data-theme');
+  document.documentElement.removeAttribute('style');
+  document.head.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]')?.remove();
+  vi.restoreAllMocks();
+});
+
+describe('theme preferences', () => {
+  it('defaults to system and accepts only known stored preferences', () => {
+    expect(readThemePreference()).toBe('system');
+
+    for (const preference of ['light', 'dark', 'system'] as const) {
+      localStorage.setItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, preference);
+      expect(readThemePreference()).toBe(preference);
+    }
+
+    localStorage.setItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, 'sepia');
+    expect(readThemePreference()).toBe('system');
+  });
+
+  it('keeps theme controls usable when browser storage is blocked', () => {
+    vi.spyOn(Storage.prototype, 'getItem').mockImplementation(() => {
+      throw new DOMException('Storage blocked', 'SecurityError');
+    });
+    const setItem = vi.spyOn(Storage.prototype, 'setItem').mockImplementation(() => {
+      throw new DOMException('Storage blocked', 'SecurityError');
+    });
+
+    expect(readThemePreference()).toBe('system');
+    expect(() => writeThemePreference('dark')).not.toThrow();
+    expect(setItem).toHaveBeenCalledWith(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, 'dark');
+  });
+
+  it('resolves system preference and applies browser and chrome colors', () => {
+    installMatchMedia(true);
+    const meta = document.createElement('meta');
+    meta.name = 'theme-color';
+    document.head.append(meta);
+
+    expect(resolveTheme('system')).toBe('dark');
+    applyTheme('dark');
+
+    expect(document.documentElement).toHaveAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');
+    expect(document.documentElement.style.colorScheme).toBe('dark');
+    expect(meta).toHaveAttribute('content', '#0f1522');
+
+    applyTheme('light');
+    expect(document.documentElement).toHaveAttribute('data-theme', 'light');
+    expect(document.documentElement.style.colorScheme).toBe('light');
+    expect(meta).toHaveAttribute('content', '#f5f3ed');
+  });
+});
+
+describe('theme bootstrap', () => {
+  it('uses the saved light preference before React starts', () => {
+    installMatchMedia(true);
+    localStorage.setItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, 'light');
+    const meta = document.createElement('meta');
+    meta.name = 'theme-color';
+    document.head.append(meta);
+
+    runBootstrap();
+
+    expect(document.documentElement).toHaveAttribute('data-theme', 'light');
+    expect(document.documentElement.style.colorScheme).toBe('light');
+    expect(meta).toHaveAttribute('content', '#f5f3ed');
+  });
+
+  it('follows a dark system when no valid preference is stored', () => {
+    installMatchMedia(true);
+    localStorage.setItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, 'invalid');
+
+    runBootstrap();
+
+    expect(document.documentElement).toHaveAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');
+    expect(document.documentElement.style.colorScheme).toBe('dark');
+  });
+
+  it('still applies the system theme when storage cannot be read', () => {
+    installMatchMedia(true);
+    vi.spyOn(Storage.prototype, 'getItem').mockImplementation(() => {
+      throw new DOMException('Storage blocked', 'SecurityError');
+    });
+
+    expect(runBootstrap).not.toThrow();
+    expect(document.documentElement).toHaveAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');
+  });
+});

+export type ThemePreference = 'light' | 'dark' | 'system';
+export type ResolvedTheme = Exclude<ThemePreference, 'system'>;
+
+export const THEME_STORAGE_KEY = 'auth-classroom:theme:v1';
+
+const themeColors: Record<ResolvedTheme, string> = {
+  light: '#f5f3ed',
+  dark: '#0f1522',
+};
+
+export function readThemePreference(): ThemePreference {
+  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
+    return 'system';
+  }
+
+  try {
+    const stored = window.localStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY);
+    return stored === 'light' || stored === 'dark' || stored === 'system'
+      ? stored
+      : 'system';
+  } catch {
+    return 'system';
+  }
+}
+
+export function writeThemePreference(preference: ThemePreference): void {
+  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
+    return;
+  }
+
+  try {
+    window.localStorage.setItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, preference);
+  } catch {
+    return;
+  }
+}
+
+export function getSystemTheme(): ResolvedTheme {
+  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || typeof window.matchMedia !== 'function') {
+    return 'light';
+  }
+
+  return window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches ? 'dark' : 'light';
+}
+
+export function resolveTheme(
+  preference: ThemePreference,
+  systemTheme: ResolvedTheme = getSystemTheme(),
+): ResolvedTheme {
+  return preference === 'system' ? systemTheme : preference;
+}
+
+export function applyTheme(theme: ResolvedTheme): void {
+  if (typeof document === 'undefined') {
+    return;
+  }
+
+  document.documentElement.dataset.theme = theme;
+  document.documentElement.style.colorScheme = theme;
+  document
+    .querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]')
+    ?.setAttribute('content', themeColors[theme]);
+}

+++ b/src/lib/themeStyles.test.ts

+import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import sourceStyles from '../index.css?raw';
+
+function tokens(theme: 'light' | 'dark') {
+  const selector = theme === 'dark'
+    ? /:root\[data-theme=["']dark["']\]\s*\{([^}]*)\}/
+    : /:root\s*\{([^}]*)\}/;
+  const block = sourceStyles.match(selector)?.[1];
+  if (!block) {
+    throw new Error(`${theme} theme tokens were not found.`);
+  }
+
+  return Object.fromEntries(
+    Array.from(block.matchAll(/--([\w-]+):\s*(#[\da-f]{6})/gi), (match) => [
+      match[1],
+      match[2],
+    ]),
+  );
+}
+
+function luminance(hex: string) {
+  const channels = [1, 3, 5].map((offset) => {
+    const value = Number.parseInt(hex.slice(offset, offset + 2), 16) / 255;
+    return value <= 0.04045 ? value / 12.92 : ((value + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4;
+  });
+  return channels[0] * 0.2126 + channels[1] * 0.7152 + channels[2] * 0.0722;
+}
+
+function contrast(foreground: string, background: string) {
+  const a = luminance(foreground);
+  const b = luminance(background);
+  return (Math.max(a, b) + 0.05) / (Math.min(a, b) + 0.05);
+}
+
+describe.each(['light', 'dark'] as const)('%s theme contrast', (theme) => {
+  it('keeps body, secondary, brand, and status text at AA contrast', () => {
+    const color = tokens(theme);
+    const pairs: Array<[string, string]> = [
+      ['ink', 'canvas'],
+      ['ink', 'surface'],
+      ['muted', 'canvas'],
+      ['muted', 'surface'],
+      ['brand', 'canvas'],
+      ['brand', 'surface'],
+      ['danger', 'soft-coral'],
+      ['coral', 'soft-coral'],
+      ['success', 'soft-success'],
+    ];
+
+    for (const [foreground, background] of pairs) {
+      expect(color[foreground], `${theme} --${foreground}`).toBeDefined();
+      expect(color[background], `${theme} --${background}`).toBeDefined();
+      expect(
+        contrast(color[foreground], color[background]),
+        `${theme} --${foreground} on --${background}`,
+      ).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5);
+    }
+  });
+
+  it('keeps inverse panels and solid action buttons at AA contrast', () => {
+    const color = tokens(theme);
+    const pairs: Array<[string, string]> = [
+      ['on-inverse', 'inverse'],
+      ['on-accent', 'accent'],
+      ['on-accent', 'accent-hover'],
+      ['on-inverse', 'brand-solid'],
+      ['on-inverse', 'brand-solid-hover'],
+    ];
+
+    for (const [foreground, background] of pairs) {
+      expect(color[foreground], `${theme} --${foreground}`).toBeDefined();
+      expect(color[background], `${theme} --${background}`).toBeDefined();
+      expect(
+        contrast(color[foreground], color[background]),
+        `${theme} --${foreground} on --${background}`,
+      ).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5);
+    }
+  });
+});

+  });

+      '#/lesson/overview',

+      'ภาพรวมระบบ Authentication',
+    );
+  });

+      'aria-valuenow',
+      '13',
+    );

+      version: 1,
+      level: 'certificate',
+      completed: { certificate: [], diploma: [] },
+      quizScores: { certificate: 5 },
+    });

+    expect(within(map).getByRole('link', { name: 'ทบทวนแบบฝึกหัด' })).toHaveAttribute(
+      'href',
+      '#/practice',
+    );
+  });
+
+  it('preserves course-map quiz status and scores when switching tracks', () => {

+      version: 1,
+      level: 'certificate',
+      completed: { certificate: ['overview'], diploma: ['security'] },
+      quizScores: { certificate: 2, diploma: 4 },
+    });
+    renderAt();

+

+
+    map = screen.getByRole('region', { name: 'แผนภูมิหลักสูตร' });
+    expect(within(map).getByText('ประกาศนียบัตรวิชาชีพชั้นสูง (ปวส.)')).toBeVisible();
+    expect(within(map).getByText('ผ่านแล้ว 4/5')).toHaveClass('bg-soft-success', 'text-success');
+    expect(within(map).getByRole('link', { name: 'ทบทวนแบบฝึกหัด' })).toBeVisible();

+      'aria-valuenow',
+      '10',
+    );
+    expect(readProgress().quizScores).toEqual({ certificate: 2, diploma: 4 });
+    expect(readProgress().completed).toEqual({
+      certificate: ['overview'],
+      diploma: ['security'],
+    });
+
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'ปวช.' }));
+
+    map = screen.getByRole('region', { name: 'แผนภูมิหลักสูตร' });
+    expect(within(map).getByText('คะแนน 2/5')).toBeVisible();
+    expect(within(map).getByRole('link', { name: 'ทำแบบฝึกหัดใหม่' })).toBeVisible();
+  });
+
+  it('redirects an unknown route to the dashboard', async () => {
+    renderAt('/not-a-route');
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveTextContent(
+        'Login และ Register',
+      );
+    });
+    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#/');
+  });
+});

+                  <span className="mt-6 inline-flex rounded-lg border border-line bg-canvas px-2.5 py-1.5 font-mono text-[11px] font-medium text-muted">
+                    {card.tag}
+                  </span>
+                </article>
+              );
+            })}
+          </div>
+        </div>
+      </section>
+
+      <section
+        aria-labelledby="classroom-title"
+        className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-5 py-16 sm:px-8 sm:py-20"
+      >
+        <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-end">
+          <div>
+            <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold tracking-[0.2em] text-brand">
+              YOUR CLASSROOM
+            </p>
+            <h2
+              id="classroom-title"
+              className="mt-3 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.03em] sm:text-3xl"
+            >
+              ความคืบหน้าและเส้นทางของคุณ
+            </h2>
+            <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted">{trackNames[level]}</p>
+          </div>
+          <LevelSwitcher level={level} onChange={setLevel} />
+        </div>
+
+        <div className="mt-8 grid items-stretch gap-5 lg:grid-cols-[1.15fr_0.85fr]">
+          <section
+            aria-labelledby="progress-summary-title"
+            className="rounded-[1.75rem] border border-line bg-surface p-6 shadow-[0_18px_50px_-38px_rgba(23,34,59,0.5)] sm:p-7"

+              </span>
+            </div>

+                </dd>

+                  <dt className="text-xs text-muted">คะแนนแบบฝึกหัด</dt>
+                  <dd className="mt-2 text-lg font-semibold text-ink">{quizScore}/5</dd>
+                </div>
+              )}
+            </dl>
+          </section>
+
+          <ContinueCard lessons={lessons} />
+        </div>
+
+        <section aria-labelledby="course-map-title" className="mt-16 sm:mt-20">
+          <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-end">
+            <div>
+              <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold tracking-[0.2em] text-brand">
+                COURSE MAP
+              </p>
+              <h2
+                id="course-map-title"
+                className="mt-3 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.03em] sm:text-3xl"
+              >
+                แผนภูมิหลักสูตร
+              </h2>
+              <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-7 text-muted">
+                เปิดบทเรียนตามลำดับ และทำเครื่องหมายความคืบหน้าเมื่อพร้อมไปต่อ
+              </p>
+            </div>
+            <div className="flex flex-col items-start gap-3 sm:items-end">
+              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
+                {quizScore !== undefined && (
+                  <p
+                    role="status"
+                    className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold ${
+                      quizScore >= passingScore
+                        ? 'bg-soft-success text-success'
+                        : 'bg-soft-coral text-danger'
+                    }`}

+            </p>
+          </div>
+        </aside>
+      </section>
+    </>
+  );

+let originalLocks: PropertyDescriptor | undefined;
+
+function installWebLocks() {
+  Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'locks', {
+    configurable: true,
+    value: { request: async (_name: string, callback: () => unknown) => callback() },
+  });
+}
+
+function renderDemo() {
+  window.location.hash = '#/demo';
+  return render(renderApp());
+}
+
+function fillRegistration() {
+  fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('ชื่อที่แสดง'), { target: { value: account.name } });
+  fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('อีเมล'), { target: { value: account.email } });
+  fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('รหัสผ่าน'), { target: { value: account.password } });
+}
+
+function fillLogin(password = account.password) {
+  fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('อีเมล'), { target: { value: account.email } });
+  fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('รหัสผ่าน'), { target: { value: password } });
+}
+
+beforeEach(() => {
+  vi.stubGlobal('crypto', webcrypto);
+  window.localStorage.clear();
+  window.sessionStorage.clear();

+  installWebLocks();
+});
+
+afterEach(() => {
+  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
+  if (originalLocks) {
+    Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'locks', originalLocks);
+  } else {
+    Reflect.deleteProperty(navigator, 'locks');
+  }
+});

+    expect(window.localStorage.getItem(USERS_KEY)).toBeNull();

+import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type FormEvent, type SyntheticEvent } from 'react';
+import {
+  ArrowRight,
+  CircleAlert,
+  Database,
+  KeyRound,
+  LogOut,
+  RotateCcw,
+  ShieldCheck,
+  Trash2,
+  UserRound,
+} from 'lucide-react';
+import { PasswordChecklist } from '../components/PasswordChecklist';
+import {
+  loginUser,
+  logout,
+  readSession,
+  registerUser,
+  resetDemoData,
+  type DemoSession,
+} from '../lib/authDemo';
+
+type DemoMode = 'register' | 'login';
+
+const inputClassName =
+  'mt-2 min-h-12 w-full rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface px-4 text-sm text-ink outline-none transition-colors placeholder:text-muted focus:border-brand';
+
+export function DemoPage() {
+  const [mode, setMode] = useState<DemoMode>('register');
+  const [name, setName] = useState('');
+  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
+  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
+  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
+  const [error, setError] = useState('');
+  const [success, setSuccess] = useState('');
+  const [session, setSession] = useState<DemoSession | null>(() => readSession());
+  const [isResetDialogOpen, setIsResetDialogOpen] = useState(false);
+  const resetDialogRef = useRef<HTMLDialogElement>(null);
+  const resetTriggerRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
+  const resetCancelRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    const dialog = resetDialogRef.current;
+    if (!dialog) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    if (isResetDialogOpen) {
+      dialog.showModal();
+      resetCancelRef.current?.focus();
+    } else if (dialog.open) {
+      dialog.close();
+      resetTriggerRef.current?.focus();
+    }
+  }, [isResetDialogOpen]);
+
+  function switchMode(nextMode: DemoMode) {
+    setMode(nextMode);
+    setPassword('');
+    setError('');
+    setSuccess('');
+  }
+
+  async function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
+    event.preventDefault();
+    if (busy) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    setBusy(true);
+    setError('');
+    setSuccess('');
+
+    try {
+      const nextSession =
+        mode === 'register'
+          ? await registerUser({ name, email, password })
+          : await loginUser({ email, password });
+      setSession(nextSession);
+      setPassword('');
+      setSuccess(mode === 'register' ? 'สร้างบัญชีและเข้าใช้งาน Demo สำเร็จ' : 'เข้าสู่ระบบสำเร็จ');
+    } catch (caught) {
+      setError(caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : 'ไม่สามารถดำเนินการได้');
+    } finally {
+      setBusy(false);
+    }
+  }
+
+  function handleLogout() {
+    setError('');
+    try {
+      logout();
+    } catch (caught) {
+      setError(caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : 'ไม่สามารถออกจากระบบได้');
+      return;
+    }
+
+    setSession(null);
+    setPassword('');
+    switchMode('login');
+    setSuccess('ออกจากระบบแล้ว ผู้ใช้ Demo ยังคงอยู่สำหรับการเข้าสู่ระบบครั้งถัดไป');
+  }
+
+  function closeResetDialog() {
+    setIsResetDialogOpen(false);
+  }
+
+  function handleResetCancel(event: SyntheticEvent<HTMLDialogElement>) {
+    event.preventDefault();
+    closeResetDialog();
+  }
+
+  function confirmReset() {
+    closeResetDialog();
+
+    try {
+      resetDemoData();
+    } catch (caught) {
+      setSession(readSession());
+      setError(caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : 'ไม่สามารถล้างข้อมูล Demo ได้');
+      return;
+    }
+
+    setSession(null);
+    setName('');
+    setEmail('');
+    setPassword('');
+    setError('');
+    setMode('register');
+    setSuccess('ล้างข้อมูล Demo แล้ว พร้อมเริ่มทดลองใหม่');
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-6xl px-5 py-10 sm:px-8 sm:py-14">
+      <header className="max-w-3xl">
+        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-brand/15 bg-soft-brand px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-brand">
+          <ShieldCheck aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
+          LAB 04 · AUTHENTICATION
+        </span>
+        <h1 className="mt-5 text-4xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.04em] sm:text-5xl">
+          Authentication Demo
+        </h1>
+        <p className="mt-4 text-base leading-8 text-muted">
+          สมัครสมาชิก ออกจากระบบ และเข้าสู่ระบบซ้ำ เพื่อดูว่า state ของหน้าเว็บเปลี่ยนอย่างไร
+          โดยข้อมูลจะอยู่เฉพาะในเบราว์เซอร์เครื่องนี้
+        </p>
+      </header>
+
+      <aside className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-coral/25 bg-soft-coral p-5 sm:flex sm:items-start sm:gap-4">
+        <CircleAlert aria-hidden="true" className="h-6 w-6 shrink-0 text-coral" />
+        <div>
+          <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-ink">สำหรับการเรียนรู้ ไม่ใช่ระบบจริง</h2>
+          <p className="mt-1.5 text-sm leading-7 text-muted">
+            Demo นี้ไม่ใช่ production authentication รหัสผ่านถูก hash ด้วย PBKDF2 ในเบราว์เซอร์
+            แต่ระบบจริงต้องตรวจสอบอีกครั้งบน server และ Demo นี้ไม่มี JWT จริงหรือการป้องกัน server
+          </p>
+        </div>
+      </aside>
+
+      {session ? (
+        <section className="mt-8 overflow-hidden rounded-[1.75rem] border border-line bg-surface shadow-[0_24px_70px_-45px_rgba(23,34,59,0.55)]">
+          <div className="border-b border-line bg-soft-brand/60 p-6 sm:p-8">
+            <span className="inline-flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-brand-solid text-on-inverse">
+              <UserRound aria-hidden="true" className="h-6 w-6" />
+            </span>
+            <p className="mt-5 text-xs font-semibold tracking-[0.16em] text-brand">SESSION ACTIVE</p>
+            <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.03em] sm:text-3xl">
+              ทดลองใช้งานสำเร็จ
+            </h2>
+            <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-7 text-muted">
+              หน้านี้ถือว่าคุณเข้าสู่ระบบแล้ว เพราะ sessionStorage ยังมี session ที่อ่านได้
+            </p>
+          </div>
+
+          <div className="grid gap-6 p-6 sm:p-8 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_auto] lg:items-end">
+            <dl className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
+              <div className="rounded-2xl border border-line bg-canvas/60 p-4">
+                <dt className="text-xs text-muted">ชื่อที่แสดง</dt>
+                <dd className="mt-1.5 text-sm font-semibold text-ink">{session.name}</dd>
+              </div>
+              <div className="rounded-2xl border border-line bg-canvas/60 p-4">
+                <dt className="text-xs text-muted">อีเมล</dt>
+                <dd className="mt-1.5 break-all text-sm font-semibold text-ink">{session.email}</dd>
+              </div>
+            </dl>
+            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">
+              <button
+                type="button"
+                onClick={handleLogout}
+                className="inline-flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-inverse px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-on-inverse transition-colors hover:bg-brand-solid-hover"
+              >
+                <LogOut aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
+                ออกจากระบบ
+              </button>
+              <button
+                ref={resetTriggerRef}
+                type="button"
+                onClick={() => setIsResetDialogOpen(true)}
+                className="inline-flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-ink transition-colors hover:border-coral/40 hover:text-coral"
+              >
+                <Trash2 aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
+                ล้างข้อมูล Demo
+              </button>
+            </div>
+          </div>
+          {error && (
+            <div role="alert" className="flex items-start gap-2 border-t border-coral/25 bg-soft-coral px-6 py-4 text-sm text-danger sm:px-8">
+              <CircleAlert aria-hidden="true" className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
+              <span>{error}</span>
+            </div>
+          )}
+          {success && (
+            <p role="status" className="border-t border-line bg-canvas/60 px-6 py-4 text-sm text-brand sm:px-8">
+              {success}
+            </p>
+          )}
+        </section>
+      ) : (
+        <div className="mt-8 grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.1fr)_minmax(0,0.9fr)]">
+          <section className="rounded-[1.75rem] border border-line bg-surface p-6 shadow-[0_24px_70px_-45px_rgba(23,34,59,0.55)] sm:p-8">
+            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 rounded-xl border border-line bg-canvas p-1" aria-label="เลือกโหมด">
+              <button
+                type="button"
+                onClick={() => switchMode('register')}
+                aria-pressed={mode === 'register'}
+                className={`min-h-11 rounded-lg px-3 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors ${
+                  mode === 'register' ? 'bg-brand-solid text-on-inverse' : 'text-muted hover:text-ink'
+                }`}
+              >
+                สมัครสมาชิก
+              </button>
+              <button
+                type="button"
+                onClick={() => switchMode('login')}
+                aria-pressed={mode === 'login'}
+                className={`min-h-11 rounded-lg px-3 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors ${
+                  mode === 'login' ? 'bg-brand-solid text-on-inverse' : 'text-muted hover:text-ink'
+                }`}
+              >
+                เข้าสู่ระบบ
+              </button>
+            </div>
+
+            <div className="mt-6">
+              <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.03em]">
+                {mode === 'register' ? 'สร้างบัญชีทดลอง' : 'กลับมาทดลองต่อ'}
+              </h2>
+              <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-7 text-muted">
+                {mode === 'register'
+                  ? 'ใช้ข้อมูลสมมติได้ ไม่ควรใช้รหัสผ่านจริงที่คุณใช้อยู่ที่อื่น'
+                  : 'ใช้อีเมลและรหัสผ่านที่สมัครไว้ใน Demo นี้'}
+              </p>
+            </div>
+
+            <form className="mt-6 space-y-5" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
+              {mode === 'register' && (
+                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-ink">
+                  ชื่อที่แสดง
+                  <input
+                    className={inputClassName}
+                    type="text"
+                    autoComplete="name"
+                    value={name}
+                    onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)}
+                    minLength={2}
+                    maxLength={80}
+                    required
+                    disabled={busy}
+                  />
+                </label>
+              )}
+              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-ink">
+                อีเมล
+                <input
+                  className={inputClassName}
+                  type="email"
+                  autoComplete="email"
+                  value={email}
+                  onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.target.value)}
+                  maxLength={255}
+                  required
+                  disabled={busy}
+                />
+              </label>
+              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-ink">
+                รหัสผ่าน
+                <input
+                  className={inputClassName}
+                  type="password"
+                  autoComplete={mode === 'register' ? 'new-password' : 'current-password'}
+                  value={password}
+                  onChange={(event) => setPassword(event.target.value)}
+                  minLength={8}
+                  maxLength={72}
+                  required
+                  disabled={busy}
+                />
+              </label>
+
+              {mode === 'register' && <PasswordChecklist password={password} />}
+
+              {error && (
+                <div role="alert" className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-xl border border-coral/25 bg-soft-coral p-4 text-sm text-danger">
+                  <CircleAlert aria-hidden="true" className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
+                  <span>{error}</span>
+                </div>
+              )}
+              {success && (
+                <p role="status" className="rounded-xl border border-brand/20 bg-soft-brand p-4 text-sm text-brand">
+                  {success}
+                </p>
+              )}
+
+              <button
+                type="submit"
+                disabled={busy}
+                className="inline-flex min-h-12 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-brand-solid px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-on-inverse transition-colors hover:bg-brand-solid-hover disabled:cursor-wait disabled:opacity-60"
+              >
+                {busy ? (
+                  <RotateCcw aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
+                ) : (
+                  <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
+                )}
+                {busy
+                  ? 'กำลังดำเนินการ...'
+                  : mode === 'register'
+                    ? 'สร้างบัญชี Demo'
+                    : 'เข้าสู่ระบบเพื่อทดลอง'}
+              </button>
+            </form>
+          </section>
+
+          <aside className="space-y-5">
+            <section className="rounded-[1.75rem] border border-line bg-inverse p-6 text-on-inverse sm:p-7">
+              <KeyRound aria-hidden="true" className="h-6 w-6 text-accent" />
+              <h2 className="mt-5 text-xl font-semibold">เกิดอะไรขึ้นเบื้องหลัง?</h2>
+              <ol className="mt-5 space-y-5">
+                {[
+                  ['01', 'ตรวจสอบข้อมูล', 'ชื่อ อีเมล และนโยบายรหัสผ่านถูกตรวจก่อนบันทึก'],
+                  ['02', 'Hash ด้วย salt', 'PBKDF2-SHA256 120,000 รอบ และ salt สุ่มต่อผู้ใช้'],
+                  ['03', 'เก็บ demo users', 'localStorage เก็บ salt และ hash ไม่เก็บรหัสผ่านตรง'],
+                  ['04', 'เปิด session', 'sessionStorage เก็บข้อมูลผู้ใช้ที่จำเป็นต่อ tab นี้'],
+                ].map(([number, title, description]) => (
+                  <li key={number} className="flex gap-4">
+                    <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-accent">{number}</span>
+                    <div>
+                      <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{title}</p>
+                      <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-6 text-on-inverse/65">{description}</p>
+                    </div>
+                  </li>
+                ))}
+              </ol>
+            </section>
+            <section className="rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-5">
+              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
+                <Database aria-hidden="true" className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-brand" />
+                <div>
+                  <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold">ข้อมูลจะอยู่ที่ไหน?</h2>
+                  <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs leading-6 text-muted">
+                    ผู้ใช้ Demo อยู่ใน localStorage จึงกลับมาเข้าสู่ระบบได้ ส่วน session อยู่ใน
+                    sessionStorage ดังนั้นการปิด tab จะออกจากระบบ แต่ผู้ใช้ยังอยู่
+                  </p>
+                </div>
+              </div>
+            </section>
+          </aside>
+        </div>
+      )}
+
+      <dialog
+        ref={resetDialogRef}
+        role="alertdialog"
+        aria-modal="true"
+        aria-labelledby="reset-demo-title"
+        aria-describedby="reset-demo-description"
+        onCancel={handleResetCancel}
+        className="m-auto w-[calc(100%-2rem)] max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-0 text-ink shadow-2xl backdrop:bg-inverse/60"
+      >
+        <div className="p-6 sm:p-7">
+          <h2 id="reset-demo-title" className="text-xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.025em]">
+            ยืนยันการล้างข้อมูล Demo
+          </h2>
+          <p id="reset-demo-description" className="mt-3 text-sm leading-7 text-muted">
+            ลบผู้ใช้ทดสอบทั้งหมดและ session ปัจจุบันจากเบราว์เซอร์นี้หรือไม่?
+          </p>
+          <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
+            <button
+              ref={resetCancelRef}
+              type="button"
+              onClick={closeResetDialog}
+              className="inline-flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-ink transition-colors hover:bg-canvas"
+            >
+              ยกเลิกการล้างข้อมูล
+            </button>
+            <button
+              type="button"
+              onClick={confirmReset}
+              className="inline-flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-danger px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-surface transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
+            >
+              ยืนยันการล้างข้อมูล
+            </button>
+          </div>
+        </div>
+      </dialog>
+    </div>
+  );
+}

+import { fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
+import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+import { renderApp } from '../App';
+import { getLessonContent } from '../data/course';
+import { readProgress, saveProgress } from '../lib/progress';
+
+const scrollIntoView = vi.fn();
+
+function renderLesson(lessonId: string) {
+  window.location.hash = `#/lesson/${lessonId}`;
+  return render(renderApp());
+}
+
+beforeEach(() => {
+  localStorage.clear();
+  window.location.hash = '#/';
+  scrollIntoView.mockClear();
+  Object.defineProperty(Element.prototype, 'scrollIntoView', {
+    configurable: true,
+    value: scrollIntoView,
+  });
+});
+
+afterEach(() => {
+  window.location.hash = '#/';
+  localStorage.clear();
+  scrollIntoView.mockClear();
+  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
+});
+
+describe('LessonPage', () => {
+  it('shows the summary, outcomes, real lesson content, and current outline item', async () => {
+    saveProgress({
+      version: 1,
+      level: 'certificate',
+      completed: { certificate: ['overview'], diploma: [] },
+      quizScores: {},
+    });
+    renderLesson('overview');
+
+    expect(screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'เส้นทางนำทาง' })).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.getByText('หลักสูตรปวช.')).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.getByText('บทที่ 1')).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveTextContent(
+      'ภาพรวมระบบ Authentication',
+    );
+    expect(screen.getByText('10 นาที')).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.getByText(/เข้าใจ flow ของ Register, Login และ Session/)).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.getByText('Authentication', { selector: 'span' })).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.getByText('Flow', { selector: 'span' })).toBeVisible();
+
+    const lesson = getLessonContent('certificate', 'overview')!;
+    const outcomes = screen.getByRole('list', { name: 'ผลลัพธ์การเรียนรู้' });
+    expect(within(outcomes).getAllByRole('listitem')).toHaveLength(3);
+    expect(document.getElementById('lesson-content')).not.toBeNull();
+    expect(document.getElementById('lesson-checkpoint')).not.toBeNull();
+    expect(screen.getByText(lesson.intro)).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.getByRole('list', { name: 'ภาพรวมเส้นทางข้อมูล' })).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.queryByText(/กำลังเตรียมเนื้อหาอธิบายและตัวอย่าง/i)).toBeNull();
+
+    const contents = screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'เนื้อหาในบทเรียน' });
+    expect(within(contents).getAllByRole('button')).toHaveLength(lesson.sections.length);
+    const requestExplorer = screen.getByRole('region', { name: 'โค้ด examples/register-request.http' });
+    expect(requestExplorer).toBeVisible();
+    expect(within(requestExplorer).getByRole('heading', { name: 'บรรทัด 1' })).toBeVisible();
+    expect(within(requestExplorer).getByText('ระบุ method POST, path /api/auth/register และ HTTP/1.1 เพื่อส่งคำขอสร้างผู้ใช้ไปยัง Backend')).toBeVisible();
+
+    const checkpoint = document.getElementById('lesson-checkpoint')!;
+    expect(within(checkpoint).getByText(lesson.checkpoint.summary)).toBeVisible();
+    expect(
+      within(screen.getByRole('list', { name: 'เช็กลิสต์จุดตรวจความเข้าใจ' })).getAllByRole('listitem'),
+    ).toHaveLength(lesson.checkpoint.checklist.length);
+
+    const outline = screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'ลำดับบทเรียน' });
+    expect(within(outline).getAllByRole('listitem')).toHaveLength(8);
+    expect(
+      within(screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'การนำทางระหว่างบทเรียน' })).getByRole('link', {
+        name: /^บทถัดไป:/,
+      }),
+    ).toHaveClass('bg-inverse', 'text-on-inverse');
+    expect(
+      within(screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'ลำดับบทเรียน' })).getByRole('link', {
+        name: /1\. ภาพรวม/,
+      }),
+    ).toHaveAttribute('aria-current', 'page');
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(readProgress().lastLessonId).toBe('overview');
+    });
+    expect(readProgress().completed.certificate).toEqual(['overview']);
+  });
+
+  it('shows diploma trade-offs only on the diploma track', () => {
+    saveProgress({
+      version: 1,
+      level: 'diploma',
+      completed: { certificate: [], diploma: [] },
+      quizScores: {},
+    });
+    const diplomaLesson = getLessonContent('diploma', 'overview')!;
+    renderLesson('overview');
+
+    expect(screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'ลำดับบทเรียน' }).querySelectorAll('li')).toHaveLength(10);
+    const diplomaNote = diplomaLesson.sections.find((section) => section.diplomaNote)!.diplomaNote!;
+    expect(screen.getByText(diplomaNote)).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.getByText('หลักสูตรปวส.')).toBeVisible();
+  });
+
+  it('moves the contents outline selection and focus without IntersectionObserver', () => {
+    vi.stubGlobal('IntersectionObserver', undefined);
+    renderLesson('overview');
+    const contents = screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'เนื้อหาในบทเรียน' });
+    const target = within(contents).getByRole('button', {
+      name: 'HTTP, JSON และสัญญาร่วมระหว่าง Frontend/Backend',
+    });
+
+    fireEvent.click(target);
+
+    expect(target).toHaveAttribute('aria-current', 'location');
+    expect(document.getElementById('http-json-contract-title')).toHaveFocus();
+    expect(scrollIntoView).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ block: 'start' });
+  });
+
+  it('uses rootMargin units accepted by native IntersectionObserver', () => {
+    class StrictObserver {
+      constructor(_callback: IntersectionObserverCallback, options?: IntersectionObserverInit) {
+        if (options?.rootMargin?.includes('rem')) {
+          throw new SyntaxError('rootMargin must be specified in pixels or percent');
+        }
+      }
+      observe() {}
+      disconnect() {}
+      unobserve() {}
+      takeRecords = () => [];
+    }
+    vi.stubGlobal('IntersectionObserver', StrictObserver);
+
+    renderLesson('overview');
+
+    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveTextContent(
+      'ภาพรวมระบบ Authentication',
+    );
+    expect(document.getElementById('lesson-content')).toBeVisible();
+  });
+
+  it('updates the active section from IntersectionObserver entries', async () => {
+    let callback: IntersectionObserverCallback | undefined;
+    const observe = vi.fn();
+    const disconnect = vi.fn();
+    class Observer {
+      constructor(observerCallback: IntersectionObserverCallback) {
+        callback = observerCallback;
+      }
+      observe = observe;
+      disconnect = disconnect;
+      unobserve = vi.fn();
+      takeRecords = vi.fn(() => []);
+    }
+    vi.stubGlobal('IntersectionObserver', Observer);
+    renderLesson('overview');
+
+    expect(observe).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(getLessonContent('certificate', 'overview')!.sections.length);
+    const section = document.getElementById('http-json-contract')!;
+    callback!(
+      [
+        {
+          target: section,
+          isIntersecting: true,
+          boundingClientRect: { top: 120 },
+        } as unknown as IntersectionObserverEntry,
+      ],
+      {} as IntersectionObserver,
+    );
+
+    const contents = screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'เนื้อหาในบทเรียน' });
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(
+        within(contents).getByRole('button', {
+          name: 'HTTP, JSON และสัญญาร่วมระหว่าง Frontend/Backend',
+        }),
+      ).toHaveAttribute('aria-current', 'location');
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('copies lesson code and shows visible feedback', async () => {
+    const originalClipboard = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(navigator, 'clipboard');
+    const writeText = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined);
+    Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'clipboard', { configurable: true, value: { writeText } });
+
+    try {
+      const lesson = getLessonContent('certificate', 'overview')!;
+      renderLesson('overview');
+      const explorer = screen.getByRole('region', { name: 'โค้ด examples/register-request.http' });
+      fireEvent.click(within(explorer).getByRole('button', { name: 'คัดลอกโค้ด' }));
+
+      expect(await within(explorer).findByText('คัดลอกแล้ว')).toBeVisible();
+      expect(writeText).toHaveBeenCalledWith(lesson.sections[1].snippets![0].code);
+    } finally {
+      if (originalClipboard) {
+        Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'clipboard', originalClipboard);
+      } else {
+        Reflect.deleteProperty(navigator, 'clipboard');
+      }
+    }
+  });
+
+  it('toggles completion through shared progress and immediately updates the dashboard', async () => {
+    renderLesson('overview');
+
+    const toggle = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'ทำเครื่องหมายว่าครบแล้ว' });
+    expect(toggle).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
+    expect(toggle).toHaveClass('bg-brand-solid', 'text-on-inverse');
+    fireEvent.click(toggle);
+
+    const cancel = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'ยกเลิกเครื่องหมายความคืบหน้า' });
+    expect(cancel).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(readProgress().completed.certificate).toEqual(['overview']);
+    });
+
+    const breadcrumbs = screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'เส้นทางนำทาง' });
+    fireEvent.click(
+      within(breadcrumbs).getByRole('link', { name: 'กลับไปยังแผนภูมิหลักสูตร' }),
+    );
+    expect(screen.getByRole('progressbar', { name: 'ความคืบหน้า' })).toHaveAttribute(
+      'aria-valuenow',
+      '13',
+    );
+    expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'เรียนต่อ' })).toHaveAttribute(
+      'href',
+      '#/lesson/installation',
+    );
+
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'เรียนต่อ' }));
+    fireEvent.click(
+      screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'ทำเครื่องหมายว่าครบแล้ว' }),
+    );
+    fireEvent.click(
+      screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'ยกเลิกเครื่องหมายความคืบหน้า' }),
+    );
+    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'ทำเครื่องหมายว่าครบแล้ว' })).toBeVisible();
+  });
+
+  it('links to the previous and next lessons inside the active track', () => {
+    renderLesson('installation');
+
+    expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: /บทก่อนหน้า: ภาพรวม/ })).toHaveAttribute(
+      'href',
+      '#/lesson/overview',
+    );
+    const next = screen.getByRole('link', { name: /บทถัดไป: โครงโปรเจกต์/ });
+    expect(next).toHaveAttribute('href', '#/lesson/setup');
+    fireEvent.click(next);
+
+    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveTextContent(
+      'จัดโครงโปรเจกต์และตั้งค่า Environment',
+    );
+  });
+
+  it('moves focus to the next lesson main content and resets scroll', async () => {
+    const scrollTo = vi.spyOn(window, 'scrollTo').mockImplementation(() => undefined);
+    renderLesson('installation');
+
+    try {
+      const next = screen.getByRole('link', { name: /บทถัดไป: โครงโปรเจกต์/ });
+      next.focus();
+      fireEvent.click(next);
+
+      await waitFor(() => {
+        expect(document.getElementById('main-content')).toHaveFocus();
+        expect(scrollTo).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ top: 0, behavior: 'instant' });
+      });
+      expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#/lesson/setup');
+      expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveTextContent(
+        'จัดโครงโปรเจกต์และตั้งค่า Environment',
+      );
+    } finally {
+      scrollTo.mockRestore();
+    }
+  });
+
+  it('uses the breadcrumb fragment to focus and scroll the course map', async () => {
+    const scrollTo = vi.spyOn(window, 'scrollTo').mockImplementation(() => undefined);
+    renderLesson('overview');
+
+    try {
+      scrollTo.mockClear();
+      const breadcrumbs = screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'เส้นทางนำทาง' });
+      const breadcrumb = within(breadcrumbs).getByRole('link', {
+        name: 'กลับไปยังแผนภูมิหลักสูตร',
+      });
+      expect(breadcrumb).toHaveAttribute('href', '#/#course-map');
+      fireEvent.click(breadcrumb);
+
+      await waitFor(() => {
+        expect(document.getElementById('course-map')).toHaveFocus();
+        expect(scrollIntoView).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ block: 'start' });
+      });
+      expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#/#course-map');
+      expect(scrollIntoView.mock.instances.at(-1)).toBe(document.getElementById('course-map'));
+      expect(scrollTo).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+    } finally {
+      scrollTo.mockRestore();
+    }
+  });
+
+  it.each([
+    ['certificate', 'testing'],
+    ['diploma', 'devops'],
+  ] as const)('sends the final %s lesson to practice', (level, lessonId) => {
+    saveProgress({
+      version: 1,
+      level,
+      completed: { certificate: [], diploma: [] },
+      quizScores: {},
+    });
+    renderLesson(lessonId);
+
+    const practice = screen.getByRole('link', { name: /ไปยังฝึกท้ายบท/ });
+    expect(practice).toHaveAttribute('href', '#/practice');
+    fireEvent.click(practice);
+    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveTextContent(
+      'ฝึกท้ายบทAuthentication',
+    );
+  });
+
+  it('redirects a diploma-only lesson when the certificate track is active', async () => {
+    renderLesson('devops');
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveTextContent(
+        'Login และ Register',
+      );
+    });
+    expect(screen.queryByRole('heading', { name: /Docker และ CI\/CD/ })).toBeNull();
+    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#/');
+  });
+
+  it('redirects an unknown lesson id to the dashboard', async () => {
+    renderLesson('unknown-lesson');
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveTextContent(
+        'Login และ Register',
+      );
+    });
+    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#/');
+  });
+
+  it('opens the working authentication demo from the dashboard', () => {
+    window.location.hash = '#/';

+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('link', { name: /ดู Demo Authentication/i }));
+

+      'Authentication Demo',
+    );
+    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'สร้างบัญชี Demo' })).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.getByText(/Demo นี้ไม่ใช่ production authentication/i)).toBeVisible();
+  });
+});

+import {
+  ArrowLeft,
+  ArrowRight,
+  BookOpenCheck,
+  Check,
+  CheckCircle2,
+  Clock3,
+  ListChecks,
+} from 'lucide-react';
+import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
+import { Link, Navigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
+import { Callout } from '../components/Callout';
+import { CodeExplorer } from '../components/CodeExplorer';
+import { useClassroomProgress } from '../context/ProgressContext';
+import { getLessonContent, getLessonsForLevel } from '../data/course';
+import type { LessonId } from '../types/course';
+
+export function LessonPage() {
+  const { lessonId } = useParams();
+  const { level, progress, toggleLesson, visitLesson } = useClassroomProgress();
+  const lessons = getLessonsForLevel(level);
+  const lesson = getLessonContent(level, lessonId as LessonId);
+  const [activeSectionId, setActiveSectionId] = useState('');
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (lesson) {
+      visitLesson(lesson.id);
+    }
+  }, [lesson, visitLesson]);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (!lesson) return;
+    setActiveSectionId(lesson.sections[0]?.id ?? '');
+    if (typeof IntersectionObserver === 'undefined') return;
+
+    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
+      (entries) => {
+        const visible = entries
+          .filter((entry) => entry.isIntersecting)
+          .sort((a, b) => a.boundingClientRect.top - b.boundingClientRect.top);
+        if (visible[0]) {
+          setActiveSectionId(visible[0].target.id);
+        }
+      },
+      { rootMargin: '-112px 0px -65% 0px', threshold: [0, 0.25, 1] },
+    );
+
+    for (const section of lesson.sections) {
+      const element = document.getElementById(section.id);
+      if (element) observer.observe(element);
+    }
+
+    return () => observer.disconnect();
+  }, [lesson]);
+
+  if (!lesson) {
+    return <Navigate to="/" replace />;
+  }
+
+  function openSection(sectionId: string) {
+    setActiveSectionId(sectionId);
+    document.getElementById(sectionId)?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'start' });
+    document.getElementById(`${sectionId}-title`)?.focus({ preventScroll: true });
+  }
+
+  const currentIndex = lessons.findIndex((candidate) => candidate.id === lesson.id);
+  const previousLesson = lessons[currentIndex - 1];
+  const nextLesson = lessons[currentIndex + 1];
+  const completed = progress.completed[level].includes(lesson.id);
+  const levelLabel = level === 'diploma' ? 'หลักสูตรปวส.' : 'หลักสูตรปวช.';
+
+  return (
+    <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-5 py-8 sm:px-8 sm:py-12">
+      <nav aria-label="เส้นทางนำทาง" className="text-sm">
+        <ol className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-muted">
+          <li>
+            <Link
+              to="/#course-map"
+              className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center gap-2 rounded-lg font-medium hover:text-brand"
+            >
+              <ArrowLeft aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
+              กลับไปยังแผนภูมิหลักสูตร
+            </Link>
+          </li>
+          <li aria-hidden="true" className="text-line">
+            /
+          </li>
+          <li aria-current="page" className="font-medium text-ink">
+            {lesson.shortTitle}
+          </li>
+        </ol>
+      </nav>
+
+      <header className="mt-7 rounded-[1.75rem] border border-line bg-surface p-6 shadow-[0_20px_55px_-42px_rgba(23,34,59,0.55)] sm:p-8">
+        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
+          <span className="rounded-full bg-soft-brand px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-brand">
+            {levelLabel}
+          </span>
+          <span className="rounded-full border border-line bg-canvas px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs font-semibold text-muted">
+            บทที่ {lesson.order}
+          </span>
+          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-line bg-canvas px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-muted">
+            <Clock3 aria-hidden="true" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
+            <span>{lesson.durationMinutes} นาที</span>
+          </span>
+        </div>
+
+        <h1 className="mt-6 max-w-4xl text-3xl font-semibold leading-tight tracking-[-0.035em] sm:text-4xl">
+          {lesson.title}
+        </h1>
+        <p className="mt-5 max-w-3xl text-base leading-8 text-muted">
+          {lesson.description}
+        </p>
+        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
+          {lesson.tags.map((tag) => (
+            <span
+              key={tag}
+              className="rounded-lg border border-line bg-canvas px-2.5 py-1.5 font-mono text-[11px] font-medium text-muted"
+            >
+              {tag}
+            </span>
+          ))}
+        </div>
+      </header>
+
+      <div className="mt-8 grid items-start gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_20rem]">
+        <aside className="order-first rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-5 lg:order-last lg:sticky lg:top-28">
+          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
+            <ListChecks aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4 text-brand" />
+            <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold">ลำดับบทเรียน</h2>
+          </div>
+          <nav aria-label="ลำดับบทเรียน" className="mt-4">
+            <ol className="space-y-1">
+              {lessons.map((outlineLesson) => {
+                const isCurrent = outlineLesson.id === lesson.id;
+                const isCompleted = progress.completed[level].includes(outlineLesson.id);
+
+                return (
+                  <li key={outlineLesson.id}>
+                    <Link
+                      to={`/lesson/${outlineLesson.id}`}
+                      aria-current={isCurrent ? 'page' : undefined}
+                      className={`flex min-h-11 items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-sm transition-colors ${
+                        isCurrent
+                          ? 'bg-soft-brand font-semibold text-brand'
+                          : 'text-muted hover:bg-canvas hover:text-ink'
+                      }`}
+                    >
+                      <span className="w-5 shrink-0 font-mono text-[11px]">
+                        {String(outlineLesson.order).padStart(2, '0')}.
+                      </span>
+                      <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">{outlineLesson.shortTitle}</span>
+                      {isCompleted && (
+                        <Check
+                          aria-hidden="true"
+                          className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-brand"
+                        />
+                      )}
+                    </Link>
+                  </li>
+                );
+              })}
+            </ol>
+          </nav>
+
+          <nav aria-label="เนื้อหาในบทเรียน" className="mt-6 border-t border-line pt-5">
+            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold">สารบัญในบทเรียน</h3>
+            <ol className="mt-3 space-y-1">
+              {lesson.sections.map((section) => {
+                const isActive = activeSectionId === section.id;
+                return (
+                  <li key={section.id}>
+                    <button
+                      type="button"
+                      aria-controls={section.id}
+                      aria-current={isActive ? 'location' : undefined}
+                      onClick={() => openSection(section.id)}
+                      className={`flex min-h-10 w-full items-center rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm transition-colors ${
+                        isActive
+                          ? 'bg-soft-brand font-semibold text-brand'
+                          : 'text-muted hover:bg-canvas hover:text-ink'
+                      }`}
+                    >
+                      {section.title}
+                    </button>
+                  </li>
+                );
+              })}
+            </ol>
+          </nav>
+        </aside>
+
+        <div className="order-last min-w-0 space-y-6 lg:order-first">
+          <section
+            aria-labelledby="outcomes-title"
+            className="rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-6 sm:p-7"
+          >
+            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
+              <span className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-soft-brand text-brand">
+                <BookOpenCheck aria-hidden="true" className="h-5 w-5" strokeWidth={1.8} />
+              </span>
+              <div>
+                <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold tracking-[0.18em] text-muted">
+                  LEARNING OUTCOMES
+                </p>
+                <h2 id="outcomes-title" className="mt-1 text-lg font-semibold">
+                  ผลลัพธ์การเรียนรู้
+                </h2>
+              </div>
+            </div>
+            <ul aria-label="ผลลัพธ์การเรียนรู้" className="mt-6 space-y-3">
+              {lesson.outcomes.map((outcome) => (
+                <li key={outcome} className="flex items-start gap-3 text-sm leading-7 text-ink">
+                  <CheckCircle2
+                    aria-hidden="true"
+                    className="mt-1 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-brand"
+                  />
+                  {outcome}
+                </li>
+              ))}
+            </ul>
+          </section>
+
+          <section
+            id="lesson-content"
+            aria-labelledby="lesson-content-title"
+            className="rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-6 sm:p-8"
+          >
+            <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold tracking-[0.18em] text-brand">
+              LESSON CONTENT
+            </p>
+            <h2 id="lesson-content-title" className="mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.025em]">
+              เนื้อหาบทเรียน
+            </h2>
+            <p className="mt-5 text-base leading-8 text-ink">{lesson.intro}</p>
+
+            <div className="mt-10 space-y-9">
+              {lesson.sections.map((section, sectionIndex) => (
+                <article
+                  key={section.id}
+                  id={section.id}
+                  aria-labelledby={`${section.id}-title`}
+                  className="scroll-mt-28 border-t border-line pt-8 first:border-t-0 first:pt-0"
+                >
+                  <p className="font-mono text-[11px] font-semibold text-brand">
+                    {String(sectionIndex + 1).padStart(2, '0')}
+                  </p>
+                  <h3
+                    id={`${section.id}-title`}
+                    tabIndex={-1}
+                    className="mt-2 text-xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.02em] outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-brand focus-visible:ring-offset-4"
+                  >
+                    {section.title}
