+                    >
+                      {section.flow.map((step, index) => (
+                        <li
+                          key={step.label}
+                          className="relative rounded-xl border border-line bg-canvas p-4"
+                        >
+                          <p className="font-mono text-[10px] font-semibold text-brand">
+                            STEP {index + 1}
+                          </p>
+                          <p className="mt-2 text-sm font-semibold text-ink">{step.label}</p>
+                          <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-6 text-muted">{step.detail}</p>
+                        </li>
+                      ))}
+                    </ol>
+                  )}
+
+                  {section.bullets && section.bullets.length > 0 && (
+                    <ul className="mt-5 space-y-2.5 rounded-xl bg-canvas p-4">
+                      {section.bullets.map((bullet) => (
+                        <li key={bullet} className="flex items-start gap-2.5 text-sm leading-7 text-ink">
+                          <CheckCircle2
+                            aria-hidden="true"
+                            className="mt-1 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-brand"
+                          />
+                          {bullet}
+                        </li>
+                      ))}
+                    </ul>
+                  )}
+
+                  {section.snippets && section.snippets.length > 0 && (
+                    <div className="mt-6 space-y-5">
+                      {section.snippets.map((snippet) => (
+                        <CodeExplorer key={snippet.id} snippet={snippet} />
+                      ))}
+                    </div>
+                  )}
+
+                  {section.callouts && section.callouts.length > 0 && (
+                    <div className="mt-6 space-y-3">
+                      {section.callouts.map((callout) => (
+                        <Callout key={`${callout.tone}-${callout.title}`} {...callout} />

+
+                  {level === 'diploma' && section.diplomaNote && (

+                      aria-label="หมายเหตุระดับปวส."
+                      className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-brand/20 bg-soft-brand/50 p-4"
+                    >
+                      <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold tracking-[0.16em] text-brand">
+                        DIPLOMA NOTE

+                      <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-7 text-ink">{section.diplomaNote}</p>
+                    </aside>
+                  )}
+                </article>
+              ))}
+            </div>

+

+            id="lesson-checkpoint"
+            aria-labelledby="lesson-checkpoint-title"

+          >
+            <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold tracking-[0.18em] text-coral">
+              CHECKPOINT

+              จุดตรวจความเข้าใจ
+            </h2>
+            <p className="mt-4 text-sm leading-7 text-muted">{lesson.checkpoint.summary}</p>

+              {lesson.checkpoint.checklist.map((item) => (
+                <li key={item} className="flex items-start gap-3 text-sm leading-7 text-ink">
+                  <CheckCircle2
+                    aria-hidden="true"
+                    className="mt-1 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-coral"
+                  />
+                  {item}
+                </li>
+              ))}
+            </ul>
+          </section>
+
+          <section
+            aria-labelledby="completion-title"
+            className="rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-6"
+          >
+            <h2 id="completion-title" className="text-base font-semibold">
+              บันทึกความคืบหน้า
+            </h2>
+            <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-7 text-muted">
+              {completed
+                ? 'คุณทำเครื่องหมายบทเรียนนี้ว่าเสร็จแล้ว'
+                : 'ทำเครื่องหมายเมื่อเข้าใจเนื้อหาและพร้อมไปต่อบทถัดไป'}
+            </p>
+            <button
+              type="button"
+              aria-pressed={completed}
+              onClick={() => toggleLesson(lesson.id, level)}
+              className={`mt-5 inline-flex min-h-12 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors sm:w-auto ${
+                completed
+                  ? 'border border-line bg-surface text-ink hover:bg-canvas'
+                  : 'bg-brand-solid text-on-inverse hover:bg-brand-solid-hover'
+              }`}
+            >
+              {completed ? (
+                <Check aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
+              ) : (
+                <CheckCircle2 aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
+              )}
+              {completed ? 'ยกเลิกเครื่องหมายความคืบหน้า' : 'ทำเครื่องหมายว่าครบแล้ว'}
+            </button>
+          </section>
+
+          <nav
+            aria-label="การนำทางระหว่างบทเรียน"
+            className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between"
+          >
+            {previousLesson ? (
+              <Link
+                to={`/lesson/${previousLesson.id}`}
+                className="inline-flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-ink transition-colors hover:border-brand/30 hover:text-brand"
+              >
+                <ArrowLeft aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
+                บทก่อนหน้า: {previousLesson.shortTitle}
+              </Link>
+            ) : (
+              <Link
+                to="/#course-map"
+                className="inline-flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-ink transition-colors hover:border-brand/30 hover:text-brand"
+              >
+                <ArrowLeft aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
+                กลับไปยังแผนภูมิหลักสูตร
+              </Link>
+            )}
+
+            {nextLesson ? (
+              <Link
+                to={`/lesson/${nextLesson.id}`}
+                className="inline-flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-inverse px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-on-inverse transition-colors hover:bg-brand-solid-hover"
+              >
+                บทถัดไป: {nextLesson.shortTitle}
+                <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
+              </Link>
+            ) : (
+              <Link
+                to="/practice"
+                className="inline-flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-inverse px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-on-inverse transition-colors hover:bg-brand-solid-hover"
+              >
+                ไปยังฝึกท้ายบท
+                <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
+              </Link>
+            )}
+          </nav>
+        </div>
+      </div>
+    </div>
+  );
+}

+import { act, fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
+import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+import { renderApp } from '../App';
+import { quizzes } from '../data/quizzes';
+import { readProgress, saveProgress } from '../lib/progress';
+import type { Level } from '../types/course';
+
+const PROGRESS_KEY = 'auth-classroom:progress:v1';
+const scrollIntoView = vi.fn();
+
+function renderPractice() {
+  window.location.hash = '#/practice';
+  return render(renderApp());
+}
+
+function selectAnswer(level: Level, questionIndex: number, optionIndex: number) {
+  const question = quizzes[level][questionIndex];
+  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: question.options[optionIndex] }));
+}
+
+function finishQuiz(level: Level, incorrectAt?: number) {
+  const questions = quizzes[level];
+  let finalAction: HTMLElement | undefined;
+
+  for (let index = 0; index < questions.length; index += 1) {
+    const question = questions[index];
+    const optionIndex = index === incorrectAt ? (question.answer + 1) % 4 : question.answer;
+    selectAnswer(level, index, optionIndex);
+    const action = screen.getByRole('button', {
+      name: index === questions.length - 1 ? 'ดูผลลัพธ์' : 'คำถามถัดไป',
+    });
+
+    if (index === questions.length - 1) {
+      finalAction = action;
+      break;
+    }
+
+    fireEvent.click(action);
+  }
+
+  if (!finalAction) {
+    throw new Error('The final quiz action was not rendered.');
+  }
+
+  return finalAction;
+}
+
+function resultRegion() {
+  const heading = screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'ผลแบบฝึกหัด' });
+  const section = heading.closest('section');
+  if (!section) {
+    throw new Error('The quiz result section was not rendered.');
+  }
+  return within(section);
+}
+
+beforeEach(() => {
+  localStorage.clear();
+  window.location.hash = '#/practice';
+  scrollIntoView.mockClear();
+  Object.defineProperty(Element.prototype, 'scrollIntoView', {
+    configurable: true,
+    value: scrollIntoView,
+  });
+});
+
+afterEach(() => {
+  window.location.hash = '#/';
+  localStorage.clear();
+  scrollIntoView.mockClear();
+});
+

+    expect(screen.getByText('คำถามที่ 1 จาก 5')).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.getByText(/ผ่านเมื่อคะแนนอย่างน้อย 4 จาก 5/)).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.getByRole('group', { name: 'ตัวเลือกคำตอบ' })).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.getAllByRole('radio')).toHaveLength(4);
+    for (const radio of screen.getAllByRole('radio')) {
+      expect(radio).not.toBeChecked();
+      expect(radio).toBeEnabled();
+    }
+    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'คำถามถัดไป' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
+    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'ดูผลลัพธ์' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
+    expect(screen.queryByRole('heading', { name: 'ผลแบบฝึกหัด' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
+    expect(screen.queryByText(/คะแนนล่าสุด:/)).not.toBeInTheDocument();
+    expect(readProgress().quizScores.certificate).toBeUndefined();

+  it('locks a correct answer and immediately shows its explanation and Next action', () => {

+  it('does not expose a result action before all five questions are answered', () => {

+    expect(readProgress().quizScores.certificate).toBeUndefined();
+  });
+
+  it('shows the exact score, pass state and full review, then persists the score', async () => {
+    renderPractice();

+
+    const result = resultRegion();

+    const reviewItems = within(review).getAllByRole('listitem');
+    expect(reviewItems).toHaveLength(5);
+
+    questions.forEach((question, index) => {
+      const userAnswer = index === 0 ? (question.answer + 1) % 4 : question.answer;
+      expect(reviewItems[index]).toHaveTextContent(question.prompt);
+      expect(reviewItems[index]).toHaveTextContent(question.options[userAnswer]);
+      expect(reviewItems[index]).toHaveTextContent(question.options[question.answer]);
+      expect(reviewItems[index]).toHaveTextContent(question.explanation);
+    });
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(readProgress().quizScores.certificate).toBe(4);
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('resets only the in-memory attempt on retry and preserves the stored score', async () => {
+    renderPractice();

+      expect(radio).not.toBeChecked();
+      expect(radio).toBeEnabled();

+    expect(readProgress().quizScores.certificate).toBe(5);
+  });
+
+  it('restores the latest stored score after reload or remount', () => {

+      quizScores: { certificate: 4 },
+    });

+    expect(screen.queryByRole('heading', { name: 'ผลแบบฝึกหัด' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
+  });

+  });

+import { ArrowLeft, ArrowRight, RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react';
+import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
+import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
+import { LevelSwitcher } from '../components/LevelSwitcher';
+import { useClassroomProgress } from '../context/ProgressContext';
+import { getQuizQuestions, hasPassed, scoreQuiz } from '../lib/quiz';
+import type { Level } from '../types/course';
+
+const trackNames: Record<Level, string> = {
+  certificate: 'หลักสูตรปวช.',
+  diploma: 'หลักสูตรปวส.',
+};
+
+interface PracticeAttemptProps {
+  level: Level;
+  recordQuiz: (level: Level, score: number) => void;
+}
+
+function PracticeAttempt({ level, recordQuiz }: PracticeAttemptProps) {
+  const questions = getQuizQuestions(level);
+  const [questionIndex, setQuestionIndex] = useState(0);
+  const [answers, setAnswers] = useState<number[]>([]);
+  const [result, setResult] = useState<number | null>(null);
+  const questionHeadingRef = useRef<HTMLHeadingElement>(null);
+  const resultHeadingRef = useRef<HTMLHeadingElement>(null);
+  const focusTargetRef = useRef<'question' | 'result' | null>(null);
+  const submittedRef = useRef(false);
+  const question = questions[questionIndex];
+  const selectedAnswer = answers[questionIndex];
+  const isLastQuestion = questionIndex === questions.length - 1;
+  const answered = selectedAnswer !== undefined;
+  const isCorrect = selectedAnswer === question.answer;
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (focusTargetRef.current === 'question') {
+      questionHeadingRef.current?.focus();
+      focusTargetRef.current = null;
+    } else if (focusTargetRef.current === 'result' && result !== null) {
+      resultHeadingRef.current?.focus();
+      focusTargetRef.current = null;
+    }
+  }, [questionIndex, result]);
+
+  function selectAnswer(optionIndex: number) {
+    if (answered || submittedRef.current) return;
+
+    const nextAnswers = [...answers];
+    nextAnswers[questionIndex] = optionIndex;
+    setAnswers(nextAnswers);
+  }
+
+  function submitQuiz() {
+    if (
+      submittedRef.current ||
+      answers.length !== questions.length ||
+      answers.some((answer) => answer === undefined)
+    ) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const score = scoreQuiz(level, answers);
+    submittedRef.current = true;
+    focusTargetRef.current = 'result';
+    setResult(score);
+    recordQuiz(level, score);
+  }
+
+  function advance() {
+    if (!answered) return;
+
+    if (isLastQuestion) {
+      submitQuiz();
+      return;
+    }
+
+    focusTargetRef.current = 'question';
+    setQuestionIndex((current) => current + 1);
+  }
+
+  function retry() {
+    submittedRef.current = false;
+    focusTargetRef.current = 'question';
+    setAnswers([]);
+    setQuestionIndex(0);
+    setResult(null);
+  }
+
+  if (result !== null) {
+    const passed = hasPassed(result);
+
+    return (
+      <section
+        aria-labelledby="quiz-result-title"
+        className="mt-8 rounded-[1.75rem] border border-line bg-surface p-6 shadow-[0_20px_55px_-42px_rgba(23,34,59,0.55)] sm:p-8"
+      >
+        <div
+          className={`rounded-2xl border p-6 text-center sm:p-8 ${
+            passed
+              ? 'border-success-border bg-soft-success text-success'
+              : 'border-danger/30 bg-soft-coral text-danger'
+          }`}
+        >
+          <h2
+            id="quiz-result-title"
+            ref={resultHeadingRef}
+            tabIndex={-1}
+            className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.03em] focus:outline-none sm:text-3xl"
+          >
+            ผลแบบฝึกหัด
+          </h2>
+          <p className="mt-5 text-sm font-medium">คะแนนของคุณ</p>
+          <p className="mt-1 text-5xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.05em]">{result}/5</p>
+          <p className="mt-4 text-lg font-semibold">{passed ? 'ผ่านแล้ว' : 'ยังไม่ผ่าน'}</p>
+          <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-7">เกณฑ์ผ่านคือ 4 จาก 5 คะแนน</p>
+        </div>
+
+        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">
+          <button
+            type="button"
+            onClick={retry}
+            className="inline-flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-brand-solid px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-on-inverse transition-colors hover:bg-brand-solid-hover"
+          >
+            <RotateCcw aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
+            ทำแบบฝึกหัดใหม่
+          </button>
+          <Link
+            to="/#course-map"
+            className="inline-flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-line bg-canvas px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-ink transition-colors hover:border-brand/30 hover:text-brand"
+          >
+            <ArrowLeft aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
+            กลับไปยังแผนภูมิหลักสูตร
+          </Link>
+        </div>
+
+        <div className="mt-9 border-t border-line pt-7">
+          <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.025em]">ทบทวนคำตอบ</h2>
+          <ol
+            aria-label="สรุปคำตอบและคำอธิบาย"
+            className="mt-5 space-y-4"
+          >
+            {questions.map((reviewQuestion, index) => {
+              const userAnswer = answers[index];
+
+              return (
+                <li
+                  key={reviewQuestion.id}
+                  className="min-w-0 rounded-2xl border border-line bg-canvas p-5"
+                >
+                  <h3 className="break-words text-base font-semibold leading-7">
+                    {index + 1}. {reviewQuestion.prompt}
+                  </h3>
+                  <p className="mt-4 break-words text-sm leading-7 text-muted">
+                    คำตอบของคุณ:{' '}
+                    <span className="font-medium text-ink">
+                      {reviewQuestion.options[userAnswer]}
+                    </span>
+                  </p>
+                  <p className="mt-1 break-words text-sm leading-7 text-muted">
+                    คำตอบที่ถูกต้อง:{' '}
+                    <span className="font-medium text-success">
+                      {reviewQuestion.options[reviewQuestion.answer]}
+                    </span>
+                  </p>
+                  <p className="mt-3 break-words text-sm leading-7 text-muted">
+                    คำอธิบาย: {reviewQuestion.explanation}
+                  </p>
+                </li>
+              );
+            })}
+          </ol>
+        </div>
+      </section>
+    );
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <section
+      aria-labelledby="quiz-question-title"
+      className="mt-8 rounded-[1.75rem] border border-line bg-surface p-6 shadow-[0_20px_55px_-42px_rgba(23,34,59,0.55)] sm:p-8"
+    >
+      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
+        <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-brand">
+          คำถามที่ {questionIndex + 1} จาก {questions.length}
+        </p>
+        <p className="text-xs text-muted">{answered ? 'ตอบแล้ว' : 'เลือกคำตอบหนึ่งข้อ'}</p>
+      </div>
+      <div
+        aria-hidden="true"
+        className="mt-4 h-1.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-line"
+      >
+        <div
+          className="h-full rounded-full bg-brand transition-[width]"
+          style={{ width: `${((questionIndex + 1) / questions.length) * 100}%` }}
+        />
+      </div>
+
+      <h2
+        id="quiz-question-title"
+        ref={questionHeadingRef}
+        tabIndex={-1}
+        className="mt-7 break-words text-xl font-semibold leading-8 tracking-[-0.025em] focus:outline-none sm:text-2xl"
+      >
+        {question.prompt}
+      </h2>
+
+      <fieldset
+        aria-describedby={answered ? 'quiz-answer-feedback' : undefined}
+        className="mt-6 min-w-0"
+      >
+        <legend className="sr-only">ตัวเลือกคำตอบ</legend>
+        <div className="grid gap-3">
+          {question.options.map((option, optionIndex) => {
+            const isSelected = selectedAnswer === optionIndex;
+            const showCorrect = answered && optionIndex === question.answer;
+            const showIncorrect = isSelected && !isCorrect;
+
+            return (
+              <label
+                key={option}
+                htmlFor={`${level}-${question.id}-${optionIndex}`}
+                className={`flex min-w-0 cursor-pointer items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border p-4 transition-colors ${
+                  showCorrect
+                    ? 'border-success-border bg-soft-success'
+                    : showIncorrect
+                      ? 'border-danger/40 bg-soft-coral'
+                      : 'border-line bg-canvas hover:border-brand/40'
+                } ${answered ? 'cursor-default' : ''}`}
+              >
+                <input
+                  id={`${level}-${question.id}-${optionIndex}`}
+                  type="radio"
+                  name={question.id}
+                  value={optionIndex}
+                  checked={isSelected}
+                  disabled={answered}
+                  onChange={() => selectAnswer(optionIndex)}
+                  className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 accent-brand"
+                />
+                <span className="min-w-0 break-words text-sm leading-7 text-ink">
+                  {option}
+                </span>
+              </label>
+            );
+          })}
+        </div>
+      </fieldset>
+
+      <div
+        id="quiz-answer-feedback"
+        aria-live="polite"
+        aria-atomic="true"
+        className="mt-5"
+      >
+        {answered && (
+          <div
+            className={`rounded-xl border p-4 ${
+              isCorrect
+                ? 'border-success-border bg-soft-success text-success'
+                : 'border-danger/30 bg-soft-coral text-danger'
+            }`}
+          >
+            <p className="text-sm font-semibold">
+              {isCorrect ? 'ตอบถูกต้อง' : 'ตอบยังไม่ถูกต้อง'}
+            </p>
+            <p className="mt-2 break-words text-sm leading-7 text-ink">
+              {question.explanation}
+            </p>
+          </div>
+        )}
+      </div>
+
+      {answered && (
+        <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end">
+          <button
+            type="button"
+            onClick={advance}
+            className="inline-flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-brand-solid px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-on-inverse transition-colors hover:bg-brand-solid-hover"
+          >
+            {isLastQuestion ? 'ดูผลลัพธ์' : 'คำถามถัดไป'}
+            <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
+          </button>
+        </div>
+      )}
+    </section>
+  );
+}
+
+export function PracticePage() {
+  const { level, progress, setLevel, recordQuiz } = useClassroomProgress();
+  const latestScore = progress.quizScores[level];
+
+  return (
+    <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-5 py-10 sm:px-8 sm:py-14">
+      <header className="rounded-[1.75rem] border border-line bg-surface p-6 shadow-[0_20px_55px_-42px_rgba(23,34,59,0.55)] sm:p-8">
+        <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 sm:flex-row sm:items-start sm:justify-between">
+          <div className="min-w-0">
+            <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold tracking-[0.2em] text-brand">
+              KNOWLEDGE CHECK
+            </p>
+            <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.035em] sm:text-4xl">
+              ฝึกท้ายบทAuthentication
+            </h1>
+            <p className="mt-4 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-7 text-muted sm:text-base">
+              แบบฝึกหัดมี 5 คำถาม ต้องตอบให้ครบ และผ่านเมื่อคะแนนอย่างน้อย 4 จาก 5
+            </p>
+            <p className="mt-3 text-sm font-semibold text-ink">{trackNames[level]}</p>
+            {latestScore !== undefined && (
+              <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted">คะแนนล่าสุด: {latestScore}/5</p>
+            )}
+          </div>
+          <LevelSwitcher level={level} onChange={setLevel} />
+        </div>
+      </header>
+
+      <PracticeAttempt key={level} level={level} recordQuiz={recordQuiz} />
+    </div>
+  );
+}

+import { fireEvent, render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+import { renderApp } from '../App';
+import { CodeExplorer } from '../components/CodeExplorer';
+import { getLessonsForLevel } from '../data/course';
+import {
+  DEMO_SESSION_KEY,
+  DEMO_USERS_KEY,
+  type DemoUser,
+} from '../lib/authDemo';
+import type { CodeSnippet } from '../types/course';
+
+const demoUser: DemoUser = {
+  id: 'demo-user-1',
+  name: 'นักศึกษา AuthLab',
+  email: 'student@example.com',
+  salt: 'test-salt',
+  passwordHash: 'test-hash',
+  createdAt: '2026-11-26T00:00:00.000Z',
+};
+
+const snippet: CodeSnippet = {
+  id: 'accessibility-example',
+  filename: 'server.js',
+  language: 'javascript',
+  code: 'app.get("/health", (req, res) => {\n\n  res.json({ ok: true });\n});',
+  notes: [
+    'สร้างเส้นทาง GET สำหรับตรวจสอบสถานะ server',
+    'เว้นบรรทัดก่อนการตอบกลับเพื่อแยกส่วนการทำงาน',
+    'ส่งสถานะสำเร็จกลับเป็น JSON',
+    'ปิด callback และคำสั่งสร้างเส้นทาง',
+  ],
+};
+
+function renderDemo() {
+  window.location.hash = '#/demo';
+  return render(renderApp());
+}
+
+function openResetDialog() {
+  const trigger = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'ล้างข้อมูล Demo' });
+  trigger.focus();
+  fireEvent.click(trigger);
+  return {
+    trigger,
+    dialog: screen.getByRole('alertdialog', { name: 'ยืนยันการล้างข้อมูล Demo' }),
+  };
+}
+
+beforeEach(() => {
+  vi.spyOn(window, 'confirm').mockReturnValue(false);
+  window.localStorage.clear();
+  window.sessionStorage.clear();
+  window.localStorage.setItem(DEMO_USERS_KEY, JSON.stringify([demoUser]));
+  window.sessionStorage.setItem(
+    DEMO_SESSION_KEY,
+    JSON.stringify({
+      id: demoUser.id,
+      name: demoUser.name,
+      email: demoUser.email,
+      createdAt: demoUser.createdAt,
+    }),
+  );
+  window.location.hash = '#/demo';
+});
+
+describe('Accessibility contract', () => {
+  it('gives landmarks, interactive controls, and Demo form fields accessible names', () => {
+    window.localStorage.removeItem(DEMO_USERS_KEY);
+    window.sessionStorage.removeItem(DEMO_SESSION_KEY);
+    renderDemo();
+
+    expect(screen.getAllByRole('main')).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(screen.getAllByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'นำทางหลัก' })).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'ข้ามไปยังเนื้อหา' })).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.getByLabelText('ชื่อที่แสดง')).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.getByLabelText('อีเมล')).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.getByLabelText('รหัสผ่าน')).toBeVisible();
+
+    for (const control of [
+      ...screen.getAllByRole('button'),
+      ...screen.getAllByRole('link'),
+    ]) {
+      expect(control).toHaveAccessibleName();
+    }
+  });
+
+  it('names every diploma course-map link by the lesson it opens', () => {
+    window.location.hash = '#/';
+    render(renderApp());
+
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'ปวส.' }));
+
+    for (const lesson of getLessonsForLevel('diploma')) {
+      expect(
+        screen.getByRole('link', { name: `เปิดบทเรียน ${lesson.title}` }),
+      ).toBeVisible();
+    }
+  });
+
+  it('announces the selected CodeExplorer line and explanation through a polite live region', () => {
+    render(<CodeExplorer snippet={snippet} />);
+    const detail = screen.getByRole('complementary', { name: 'รายละเอียดบรรทัด' });
+    const liveRegion = detail.querySelector('[aria-live="polite"]');
+
+    expect(liveRegion).toHaveAttribute('aria-atomic', 'true');
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'บรรทัด 2' }));
+
+    expect(liveRegion).toHaveTextContent('บรรทัด 2');
+    expect(liveRegion).toHaveTextContent(snippet.notes[1]);
+    expect(within(detail).getByRole('heading', { name: 'บรรทัด 2' })).toBeVisible();
+  });
+
+  it('opens an accessible reset dialog, moves focus inside, and closes without deletion on Escape', () => {
+    renderDemo();
+    const { trigger, dialog } = openResetDialog();
+    const cancelButton = within(dialog).getByRole('button', {
+      name: 'ยกเลิกการล้างข้อมูล',
+    });
+
+    expect(dialog).toHaveAttribute('aria-modal', 'true');
+    expect(window.confirm).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+    expect(dialog).toHaveAccessibleDescription(
+      'ลบผู้ใช้ทดสอบทั้งหมดและ session ปัจจุบันจากเบราว์เซอร์นี้หรือไม่?',
+    );
+    expect(cancelButton).toHaveFocus();
+
+    const cancelEvent = new Event('cancel', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true });
+    fireEvent(dialog, cancelEvent);
+
+    expect(cancelEvent.defaultPrevented).toBe(true);
+    expect(screen.queryByRole('alertdialog')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
+    expect(trigger).toHaveFocus();
+    expect(window.localStorage.getItem(DEMO_USERS_KEY)).not.toBeNull();
+    expect(window.sessionStorage.getItem(DEMO_SESSION_KEY)).not.toBeNull();
+    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'ทดลองใช้งานสำเร็จ' })).toBeVisible();
+  });
+
+  it('keeps Demo data when reset is cancelled and deletes it only after confirmation', () => {
+    renderDemo();
+    const { trigger, dialog } = openResetDialog();
+
+    fireEvent.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'ยกเลิกการล้างข้อมูล' }));
+
+    expect(screen.queryByRole('alertdialog')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
+    expect(trigger).toHaveFocus();
+    expect(window.localStorage.getItem(DEMO_USERS_KEY)).not.toBeNull();
+    expect(window.sessionStorage.getItem(DEMO_SESSION_KEY)).not.toBeNull();
+
+    const confirmed = openResetDialog();
+    fireEvent.click(
+      within(confirmed.dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'ยืนยันการล้างข้อมูล' }),
+    );
+
+    expect(screen.queryByRole('alertdialog')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
+    expect(window.localStorage.getItem(DEMO_USERS_KEY)).toBeNull();
+    expect(window.sessionStorage.getItem(DEMO_SESSION_KEY)).toBeNull();
+    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'สร้างบัญชี Demo' })).toBeVisible();
+    expect(screen.getByRole('status')).toHaveTextContent('ล้างข้อมูล Demo แล้ว');
+  });
+});

+import { existsSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { resolve } from 'node:path';
+import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+
+describe('Application document', () => {
+  it('declares a local favicon that exists', () => {
+    const html = readFileSync(resolve('index.html'), 'utf8');
+
+    expect(html).toContain('<link rel="icon" href="/favicon.svg" />');
+    expect(existsSync(resolve('public/favicon.svg'))).toBe(true);
+  });
+});
